===== TICKET =====
KS-1384 — One anchor row per (document_id, network): a repeat POST /api/anchors resets the existing row to pending and nulls its transaction hash — so Platform-S's per-event anchoring collapses to one anchor per document (PS-895 companion) [In Progress]

## Found by

The S↔K compatibility review of 2026-09-29 (platform-s `477eb57`, platform-k `0aa9b52c6`; origin/develop `b4461d1e2` is three commits ahead, none touching this). Companion to [PS-895](https://linear.app/secuura/issue/PS-895/question-in-simulated-anchoring-six-distinct-lifecycle-events-on-one), which asked the question from the S side after seeing six lifecycle events on one document share one External Ref, and one on-chain transaction for register + declare on dev-ps.

## What the code does

`dbPersistAnchor` upserts on the composite key and rewrites the chain columns from the **new** request:

```
services/anchoring/src/index.ts:1334-1372
  INSERT INTO anchor_store (...)
  ON CONFLICT (document_id, network) DO UPDATE SET
    status = EXCLUDED.status, transaction_hash = EXCLUDED.transaction_hash,
    block_hash/block_number/slot/fee/confirmations/confirmed_at = EXCLUDED.…,
    error_message, retry_count, simulated, updated_at = EXCLUDED.…
  RETURNING id
```

`metadata_payload` and `content_hash` are **not** in the update list, so the first event's label and hash stay on the row. Every `POST /api/anchors` builds the anchor with `status: 'pending'` and a null hash (`index.ts:463-560`), so on a conflict the existing row goes back to `pending` with `transaction_hash = NULL`, and `processAnchor(persistedId)` runs again (`:535`).

K's own share, transfer-custody, revoke and lifecycle-event routes all go through `emitLifecycleAnchor`, which posts only `{documentId, contentHash, network}` to that same flat endpoint (`services/originate/src/routes/documents.ts:1275-1297`). So every non-mutating verb S records re-enters the same row for the document.

Migration 031 records the one-row-per-(document, network) decision. `anchorSubmission.ts:383-386` (guard 1) reads the row's `transaction_hash` to avoid a second fee-paying transaction — the upsert has just nulled it.

## What this means for the S↔K contract

* Platform-S's user-facing promise (`docs/USER-MANUAL.md` §1, §7) is that every declare, sign and share event is anchored. K anchors **one row per document version per network**; later events reset and re-process it.
* For every verb S routes through `/lifecycle-events`, `/share` or `/transfer-custody`, the chain record carries **no** `documentType` **and no** `identity` — `emitLifecycleAnchor` sends neither, and the lifecycle-events handler reads only `action` and `payload` (`documents.ts:2440-2545`). That is a [KS-1175](https://linear.app/secuura/issue/KS-1175/anchor-originate-lifecycle-event-schemas-accept-and-anchor-platform-ss) gap; filed separately.

## Decisions needed (Kamil)

1. Is one anchor per (document, network) the intended contract? If yes, S will label its External Ref column "anchor for version X" and group events ([PS-895](https://linear.app/secuura/issue/PS-895/question-in-simulated-anchoring-six-distinct-lifecycle-events-on-one) item 2), and the USER-MANUAL wording changes.
2. If events should each anchor, the upsert key needs an event dimension and `emitLifecycleAnchor` needs the verb.
3. Either way: **what happens on the reset.** Trace whether `processAnchor` after a conflict mints a second Cardano transaction for the same document (guard 1 cannot see the previous hash any more), or is caught by another guard. Not verified live in the review; [PS-895](https://linear.app/secuura/issue/PS-895/question-in-simulated-anchoring-six-distinct-lifecycle-events-on-one)'s dev-ps observation was one transaction for two events.

## Verify

* Unit: a second `POST /api/anchors` for the same `(documentId, network)` returns the existing id **and** either leaves a confirmed row's `transaction_hash` intact (per-document contract) or creates a distinct row (per-event contract) — whichever is ruled.
* Live on a paired slot: originate → declare → share on one document; count anchor rows and Cardano transactions; quote both on this ticket.

Related: [PS-895](https://linear.app/secuura/issue/PS-895/question-in-simulated-anchoring-six-distinct-lifecycle-events-on-one) (the S-side question), [KS-1175](https://linear.app/secuura/issue/KS-1175/anchor-originate-lifecycle-event-schemas-accept-and-anchor-platform-ss) (identity block), [PS-870](https://linear.app/secuura/issue/PS-870/6-send-the-identity-record-to-platform-k-on-every-anchored-verb).

+/**
+ * KS-1384 F-a — real-Postgres cells for the row-count builder and payloadTimestamp (Seat W 10th's matrix).
+ *
+ *   (b2) The builder's expectations, EXECUTED: a row with no hash matches `expectTransactionHash: null`
+ *        (`IS NULL`); a row whose status is not the expected one matches 0 and is left as it was.
+ *        Mutant: `transaction_hash = NULL` for the null case (never matches).
+ *   (w)  One `Date` per request (design M5): for a fresh row, the 202's payloadTimestamp, the stored
+ *        payload's `secuura.timestamp` and the row's created_at are the same instant.
+ *        Mutant: two `new Date()` calls (the payload's timestamp and the row's created_at taken apart).
+ *   (w2) A repeat request for the same flat document id, whose KS-1385 overwrite is declined (a non-uuid id
+ *        that names no document keeps the stored payload, by design), answers payloadTimestamp = the KEPT
+ *        payload's time, read in the upsert's own RETURNING, not this request's.
+ *        Mutant: answer the incoming payload's timestamp.
+ *
+ * Needs a Postgres carrying the repo schema: TEST_DATABASE_URL. It FAILS when that is unset — it never skips
+ * (workspace no-skip rule). No chain keys: anchoring runs its mock path, which never touches a network.
+ */
+import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, afterAll, vi } from 'vitest';
+import crypto from 'crypto';
+import http from 'http';
+import type { AddressInfo } from 'net';
+import express from 'express';
+import { Client } from 'pg';
+import { runAnchorStatusUpdate } from '../anchorStatusUpdate';
+
+const OWNER_URL = process.env.TEST_DATABASE_URL;
+
+const TENANT = '0a138400-0000-4000-8000-0000000138a0';
+const ORG = '1a138400-0000-4000-8000-0000000138a1';
+const USER = '2a138400-0000-4000-8000-0000000138a2';
+const HASH = 'd'.repeat(64);
+
+const { privateKey, publicKey } = crypto.generateKeyPairSync('rsa', { modulusLength: 2048 });
+const PRIVATE_PEM = privateKey.export({ type: 'pkcs8', format: 'pem' }).toString();
+const PUBLIC_PEM = publicKey.export({ type: 'spki', format: 'pem' }).toString();
+const b64url = (b: Buffer): string => b.toString('base64').replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '');
+function token(): string {
+  const header = b64url(Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ alg: 'RS256', typ: 'JWT' })));
+  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
+  const payload = b64url(Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ iat: now, exp: now + 600, role: 'user', verificationLevel: 'STANDARD', userId: USER, organizationId: ORG, tenantId: TENANT })));
+  return `${header}.${payload}.${b64url(crypto.sign('RSA-SHA256', Buffer.from(`${header}.${payload}`), PRIVATE_PEM))}`;
+}
+
+let server: http.Server;
+let base: string;
+let owner: Client;
+const madeDocIds: string[] = [];
+const madeAnchorIds: string[] = [];
+
+let seq = 0;
+function flatId(): string { seq += 1; return `doc-${Date.now()}${seq}-ks1384fa${seq}`; }
+
+async function postAnchor(documentId: string) {
+  const res = await fetch(`${base}/api/anchors`, {
+    method: 'POST',
+    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Authorization: `Bearer ${token()}` },
+    body: JSON.stringify({ documentId, contentHash: HASH }),
+  });
+  const json = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as { data?: { id?: string; payloadTimestamp?: string | null; createdAt?: string } };
+  if (json?.data?.id) madeAnchorIds.push(json.data.id);
+  return { status: res.status, json };
+}
+
+async function storedRow(documentId: string) {
+  const r = await owner.query(
+    `SELECT id, created_at, metadata_payload->'secuura'->>'timestamp' AS payload_ts FROM anchor_store WHERE document_id = $1`,
+    [documentId],
+  );
+  return r.rows;
+}
+
+beforeAll(async () => {
+  if (!OWNER_URL) {
+    throw new Error('KS-1384 F-a integration cells need TEST_DATABASE_URL (a Postgres carrying the repo schema) — refusing to pass without it');
+  }
+  owner = new Client({ connectionString: OWNER_URL });
+  await owner.connect();
+  process.env.DATABASE_URL = OWNER_URL;
+  process.env.JWT_PUBLIC_KEY = Buffer.from(PUBLIC_PEM, 'utf8').toString('base64');
+  delete process.env.JWT_JWKS_URL;
+  delete process.env.AUTH_SERVICE_URL;
+  delete process.env.PLATFORM_WALLET_MNEMONIC;
+  delete process.env.BLOCKFROST_API_KEY;
+  process.env.CARDANO_NETWORK = process.env.CARDANO_NETWORK || 'preview';
+  vi.spyOn(express.application, 'listen').mockReturnValue(
+    { keepAliveTimeout: 0, headersTimeout: 0, close: () => undefined } as unknown as http.Server,
+  );
+  const app = (await import('../index')).default;
+  const db = await import('../db');
+  await db.initDb();
+  for (let i = 0; i < 60 && !db.isDbAvailable(); i += 1) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 100));
+  if (!db.isDbAvailable()) throw new Error('anchoring did not connect — the cells below would be unfalsifiable');
+  server = http.createServer(app);
+  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => { server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', resolve); });
+  base = `http://127.0.0.1:${(server.address() as AddressInfo).port}`;
+}, 60000);
+
+afterAll(async () => {
+  if (server) await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.close(() => resolve()));
+  const db = await import('../db');
+  await db.closeDb().catch(() => undefined);
+  if (owner) {
+    if (madeDocIds.length) await owner.query('DELETE FROM anchor_store WHERE document_id = ANY($1::text[])', [madeDocIds]).catch(() => undefined);
+    if (madeAnchorIds.length) await owner.query('DELETE FROM anchor_store WHERE id = ANY($1::text[])', [madeAnchorIds]).catch(() => undefined);
+    await owner.end();
+  }
+  vi.restoreAllMocks();
+});
+
+describe('(b2) KS-1384 F-a: the builder\'s expectations, executed on Postgres', () => {
+  it('a row with no hash matches expectTransactionHash: null; a status that moved matches 0 and the row is untouched', async () => {
+    const id = `anchor_${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
+    const doc = flatId(); madeDocIds.push(doc);
+    await owner.query(
+      `INSERT INTO anchor_store (id, document_id, anchor_type, content_hash, network, metadata_payload, status, simulated)
+       VALUES ($1, $2, 'single', $3, 'preview', '{}'::jsonb, 'pending', false)`,
+      [id, doc, HASH],
+    );
+    const deps = { isDbAvailable: () => true, ensureTables: async () => undefined, query: (s: string, p?: unknown[]) => owner.query(s, p as any[]), log: () => undefined };
+
+    // CONTROL: the row is there and has no hash.
+    const before = await owner.query('SELECT status, transaction_hash, error_message FROM anchor_store WHERE id = $1', [id]);
+    expect(before.rows).toEqual([{ status: 'pending', transaction_hash: null, error_message: null }]);
+
+    // A status that is not the row's: 0 rows, nothing written.
+    await expect(runAnchorStatusUpdate(deps, id, { errorMessage: 'should not land', expectStatus: 'submitted' })).resolves.toBe(0);
+    const unchanged = await owner.query('SELECT error_message FROM anchor_store WHERE id = $1', [id]);
+    expect(unchanged.rows[0].error_message).toBeNull();
+
+    // The NULL hash expectation matches the NULL-hash row.
+    await expect(runAnchorStatusUpdate(deps, id, { errorMessage: 'landed', expectStatus: 'pending', expectTransactionHash: null })).resolves.toBe(1);
+    const after = await owner.query('SELECT error_message FROM anchor_store WHERE id = $1', [id]);
+    expect(after.rows[0].error_message).toBe('landed');
+
+    // And a real-hash expectation does NOT match a NULL-hash row.
+    await expect(runAnchorStatusUpdate(deps, id, { errorMessage: 'nope', expectTransactionHash: 'e'.repeat(64) })).resolves.toBe(0);
+  });
+});
+
+describe('(w) KS-1384 F-a: one Date per request', () => {
+  it('a fresh row: the 202\'s payloadTimestamp, the stored payload\'s timestamp and created_at are one instant', async () => {
+    const doc = flatId(); madeDocIds.push(doc);
+    const r = await postAnchor(doc);
+    expect(r.status, JSON.stringify(r.json)).toBe(202);
+    const rows = await storedRow(doc);
+    expect(rows).toHaveLength(1);
+    const ts = r.json.data?.payloadTimestamp;
+    expect(typeof ts).toBe('string');
+    expect(rows[0].payload_ts).toBe(ts);
+    expect(new Date(rows[0].created_at).toISOString()).toBe(ts);
+  });
+});
+
+describe('(w2) KS-1384 F-a: a declined overwrite reports the KEPT payload\'s time', () => {
+  it('a repeat request for the same flat id answers the first payload\'s timestamp, and the row still holds it', async () => {
+    const doc = flatId(); madeDocIds.push(doc);
+    const first = await postAnchor(doc);
+    expect(first.status, JSON.stringify(first.json)).toBe(202);
+    const firstTs = first.json.data?.payloadTimestamp as string;
+    expect(typeof firstTs).toBe('string');
+
+    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 25)); // the second request's own clock is strictly later
+    const second = await postAnchor(doc);
+    expect(second.status, JSON.stringify(second.json)).toBe(202);
+
+    // CONTROL: the overwrite really was declined — the row still holds the first payload's timestamp.
+    const rows = await storedRow(doc);
+    expect(rows).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(rows[0].payload_ts).toBe(firstTs);
+    // THE CELL: the second 202 reports the kept payload's time, not its own request's.
+    expect(second.json.data?.payloadTimestamp).toBe(firstTs);
+    // And the request's own clock did move on: its createdAt (this request's Date) is later than firstTs.
+    expect(Date.parse(String(second.json.data?.createdAt))).toBeGreaterThan(Date.parse(firstTs));
+  });
+});

+/**
+ * KS-1384 F-a — the reconciler's give-up (Seat W 10th's matrix, unit half; the real-Postgres half is
+ * ks1384-fa-reconciler-writes.integration.test.ts).
+ *
+ *   (r)   KS-1808: the 24 h window runs from the row's LAST WRITE (`updated_at`). A row created 30 days ago and
+ *         re-armed now is not failed; a row silent for 25 h is, after two answered passes.
+ *         Mutant: read `created_at`.
+ *   (t)   KS-1810: a pass in which the chain could not be asked (`polled 0, errored 1`) never fails a row; two
+ *         CONSECUTIVE answered "not found" passes past the window do; an errored pass between them, a thrown
+ *         check, or a new hash on the row starts the count again.
+ *         Mutants: read `confirmed` only (treat every not-confirmed as answered); no reset on an errored pass;
+ *         count by anchor id alone; fail on the first answered pass.
+ *   (r3s) Every reconciler write expects the status set and the hash it read, and only a write that matched
+ *         its row (1) is counted: 0 (the row moved) and null (the write did not run) are not.
+ *         Mutants: drop `expectTransactionHash`; drop `expectStatus`; count whatever the write returned.
+ */
+import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { createStuckAnchorReconciler, ANSWERED_MISSES_TO_FAIL, type ReconcilerDeps, type ReconcilerCheck } from '../reconciler';
+
+const TX_A = 'a1'.repeat(32);
+const TX_B = 'b2'.repeat(32);
+const HOUR = 3_600_000;
+const NOW = Date.parse('2026-10-30T02:00:00Z');
+const CONFIG = { minAgeMs: 10 * 60_000, giveUpMs: 24 * HOUR, batch: 25 };
+
+const ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND: ReconcilerCheck = { confirmed: false, polled: 1, errored: 0 };
+const PROVIDER_ERROR: ReconcilerCheck = { confirmed: false, polled: 0, errored: 1 };
+
+function row(over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
+  return { id: 'anchor-fa', status: 'submitted', transaction_hash: TX_A, created_at: new Date(NOW - 2 * HOUR).toISOString(), updated_at: new Date(NOW - 2 * HOUR).toISOString(), ...over };
+}
+
+/** A reconciler whose rows and chain answers are scripted per pass. */
+function harness(passes: Array<{ rows: any[]; check: ReconcilerCheck | (() => never) }>, writeResult: number | null = 1) {
+  const writes: Array<{ id: string; updates: Record<string, unknown> }> = [];
+  const log = vi.fn();
+  let pass = -1;
+  const deps: ReconcilerDeps = {
+    isDbAvailable: () => true,
+    isCardanoReady: () => true,
+    query: vi.fn(async () => { pass += 1; return { rows: passes[pass].rows }; }),
+    checkTx: vi.fn(async () => {
+      const c = passes[pass].check;
+      if (typeof c === 'function') return c();
+      return c;
+    }),
+    updateStatus: vi.fn(async (id: string, updates: Record<string, unknown>) => { writes.push({ id, updates }); return writeResult; }),
+    log,
+    now: () => NOW,
+  };
+  const run = createStuckAnchorReconciler(deps, CONFIG);
+  return { run, writes, log };
+}
+
+describe('(r) KS-1808: the give-up window runs from the last write', () => {
+  it('a row created 30 days ago and re-armed 11 minutes ago is not failed, however many answered passes', async () => {
+    const reArmed = row({ created_at: new Date(NOW - 30 * 24 * HOUR).toISOString(), updated_at: new Date(NOW - 11 * 60_000).toISOString() });
+    const { run, writes } = harness([0, 1, 2].map(() => ({ rows: [reArmed], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND })));
+    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 0 });
+    expect(writes).toHaveLength(0);
+  });
+
+  it('a row silent for 25 hours is failed at its second answered pass', async () => {
+    const silent = row({ created_at: new Date(NOW - 25 * HOUR).toISOString(), updated_at: new Date(NOW - 25 * HOUR).toISOString() });
+    const { run, writes } = harness([0, 1].map(() => ({ rows: [silent], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND })));
+    expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 0 });
+    expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 1 });
+    expect(writes).toHaveLength(1);
+    expect(writes[0].updates.status).toBe('failed');
+  });
+});
+
+// Old on BOTH clocks, so the pin's created_at reconciler would also give it up: the (t) cells then separate the
+// two rules on the answered count alone.
+const PAST = () => row({ created_at: new Date(NOW - 25 * HOUR).toISOString(), updated_at: new Date(NOW - 25 * HOUR).toISOString() });
+
+describe('(t) KS-1810: only answered "not found" passes count, and they must be consecutive', () => {
+  it('the rule needs two answered passes (the exported constant the cells below rely on)', () => {
+    expect(ANSWERED_MISSES_TO_FAIL).toBe(2);
+  });
+
+  it('a provider that never answers never fails a row past the window, and nothing is written', async () => {
+    const { run, writes, log } = harness([0, 1, 2, 3].map(() => ({ rows: [PAST()], check: PROVIDER_ERROR })));
+    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 0 });
+    expect(writes).toHaveLength(0);
+    expect(log.mock.calls.some((c) => /could not be asked/.test(String(c[1])))).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it('two consecutive answered passes fail the row at the second, not the first', async () => {
+    const { run, writes } = harness([0, 1].map(() => ({ rows: [PAST()], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND })));
+    expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
+    expect(writes).toHaveLength(0);
+    expect((await run()).failed).toBe(1);
+  });
+
+  it('answered, errored, answered: not failed (the error restarted the count); a fourth answered pass fails it', async () => {
+    const { run, writes } = harness([ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND, PROVIDER_ERROR, ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND, ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND].map((check) => ({ rows: [PAST()], check })));
+    expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
+    expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
+    expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
+    expect(writes).toHaveLength(0);
+    expect((await run()).failed).toBe(1);
+  });
+
+  it('a thrown check restarts the count too', async () => {
+    const boom = () => { throw new Error('blockfrost 429'); };
+    const { run, writes } = harness([
+      { rows: [PAST()], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND },
+      { rows: [PAST()], check: boom as () => never },
+      { rows: [PAST()], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND },
+    ]);
+    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
+    expect(writes).toHaveLength(0);
+  });
+
+  it('a new hash on the row starts the count again (the count belongs to the transaction, not the anchor id)', async () => {
+    const { run, writes } = harness([
+      { rows: [PAST()], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND },
+      { rows: [row({ transaction_hash: TX_B, created_at: new Date(NOW - 25 * HOUR).toISOString(), updated_at: new Date(NOW - 25 * HOUR).toISOString() })], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND },
+    ]);
+    expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
+    expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
+    expect(writes).toHaveLength(0);
+  });
+
+  it('inside the window an answered pass is not counted: the first answered pass after it crosses is still only the first', async () => {
+    const young = row({ updated_at: new Date(NOW - 2 * HOUR).toISOString() });
+    const { run, writes } = harness([
+      { rows: [young], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND },
+      { rows: [PAST()], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND },
+    ]);
+    expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
+    expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
+    expect(writes).toHaveLength(0);
+  });
+});
+
+describe('(r3s) every reconciler write expects what it read, and only a matched write counts', () => {
+  it('the confirmed write expects the non-final statuses and the read hash', async () => {
+    const { run, writes } = harness([{ rows: [PAST()], check: { confirmed: true, polled: 1, blockNumber: 7, slot: 70, blockHash: 'bh' } }]);
+    expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 1, failed: 0 });
+    expect(writes[0].updates).toMatchObject({ status: 'confirmed', expectStatus: ['pending', 'submitted', 'submitting'], expectTransactionHash: TX_A });
+  });
+
+  it('the failed write expects the same', async () => {
+    const { run, writes } = harness([0, 1].map(() => ({ rows: [PAST()], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND })));
+    await run(); await run();
+    expect(writes[0].updates).toMatchObject({ status: 'failed', expectStatus: ['pending', 'submitted', 'submitting'], expectTransactionHash: TX_A });
+  });
+
+  it('a confirmed write that matched 0 rows (the row moved) is not counted, and says why', async () => {
+    const { run, log } = harness([{ rows: [PAST()], check: { confirmed: true, polled: 1 } }], 0);
+    expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 0 });
+    expect(log.mock.calls.some((c) => /row moved after it was read/.test(String(c[1])))).toBe(true);
+  });
+
+  it('a failed write that did not run (null) is not counted', async () => {
+    const { run } = harness([0, 1].map(() => ({ rows: [PAST()], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND })), null);
+    await run();
+    expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 0 });
+  });
+});

+/**
+ * KS-1384 F-a — the reconciler against real Postgres (Seat W 10th's matrix).
+ *
+ *   (r2) A pass that finds the transaction not yet on chain, inside the window, writes nothing: the row's
+ *        updated_at is unchanged (so KS-1808's window, which reads updated_at, is not pushed by the reconciler
+ *        itself). Mutant: a touch in the not-yet branch.
+ *   (r3) The reconciler's write misses when the row moved to a new hash between its SELECT and its write (a
+ *        re-anchor upsert landing during the chain check): the row keeps the new hash and its status, and the
+ *        pass counts nothing. Mutant: drop `expectTransactionHash` from the reconciler's guard.
+ *
+ * The reconciler runs with its real SELECT (deps.query on Postgres) and its writes through the product's
+ * `runAnchorStatusUpdate`. Only the chain check is scripted. Needs TEST_DATABASE_URL; FAILS when unset.
+ */
+import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, afterAll } from 'vitest';
+import crypto from 'crypto';
+import { Client } from 'pg';
+import { createStuckAnchorReconciler } from '../reconciler';
+import { runAnchorStatusUpdate } from '../anchorStatusUpdate';
+
+const OWNER_URL = process.env.TEST_DATABASE_URL;
+const HASH = 'e'.repeat(64);
+const TX_OLD = 'c3'.repeat(32);
+const TX_NEW = 'd4'.repeat(32);
+const made: string[] = [];
+let owner: Client;
+
+beforeAll(async () => {
+  if (!OWNER_URL) throw new Error('KS-1384 F-a reconciler integration cells need TEST_DATABASE_URL — refusing to pass without it');
+  owner = new Client({ connectionString: OWNER_URL });
+  await owner.connect();
+});
+
+afterAll(async () => {
+  if (owner) {
+    if (made.length) await owner.query('DELETE FROM anchor_store WHERE id = ANY($1::text[])', [made]).catch(() => undefined);
+    await owner.end();
+  }
+});
+
+async function insertRow(status: string, tx: string, updatedAgo: string): Promise<string> {
+  const id = `anchor_${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
+  made.push(id);
+  await owner.query(
+    `INSERT INTO anchor_store (id, document_id, anchor_type, content_hash, network, metadata_payload, status, transaction_hash, simulated, created_at, updated_at)
+     VALUES ($1, $2, 'single', $3, 'preview', '{}'::jsonb, $4, $5, false, NOW() - $6::interval, NOW() - $6::interval)`,
+    [id, `doc-ks1384fa-${id.slice(-12)}`, HASH, status, tx, updatedAgo],
+  );
+  return id;
+}
+
+function reconcilerFor(onlyId: string, check: (tx: string) => Promise<any>) {
+  const deps = { isDbAvailable: () => true, ensureTables: async () => undefined, query: (s: string, p?: unknown[]) => owner.query(s, p as any[]), log: () => undefined };
+  return createStuckAnchorReconciler({
+    isDbAvailable: () => true,
+    isCardanoReady: () => true,
+    // The real SELECT, narrowed to this cell's row so rows from other cells or other suites cannot interfere.
+    query: async (sql: string, params?: unknown[]) => {
+      const r = await owner.query(sql, params as any[]);
+      return { rows: r.rows.filter((x: any) => x.id === onlyId) };
+    },
+    checkTx: check,
+    updateStatus: (anchorId: string, updates: Record<string, unknown>) => runAnchorStatusUpdate(deps, anchorId, updates as any),
+    log: () => undefined,
+  }, { minAgeMs: 10 * 60_000, giveUpMs: 24 * 3_600_000, batch: 10_000 });
+}
+
+describe('(r2) KS-1384 F-a: a not-yet pass writes nothing', () => {
+  it('inside the window, an answered "not found" leaves updated_at exactly as it was', async () => {
+    const id = await insertRow('submitted', TX_OLD, '2 hours');
+    const before = (await owner.query('SELECT updated_at, status, error_message FROM anchor_store WHERE id = $1', [id])).rows[0];
+    const run = reconcilerFor(id, async () => ({ confirmed: false, polled: 1, errored: 0 }));
+    // CONTROL: the row is selected (scanned 1), so a write would have been possible.
+    expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 0 });
+    const after = (await owner.query('SELECT updated_at, status, error_message FROM anchor_store WHERE id = $1', [id])).rows[0];
+    expect(new Date(after.updated_at).getTime()).toBe(new Date(before.updated_at).getTime());
+    expect(after.status).toBe('submitted');
+    expect(after.error_message).toBeNull();
+  });
+});
+
+describe('(r3) KS-1384 F-a: the reconciler\'s write misses a row that moved to a new hash', () => {
+  it('a re-anchor during the chain check: the confirmed write matches 0, the row keeps the new hash and status', async () => {
+    const id = await insertRow('submitted', TX_OLD, '2 hours');
+    const run = reconcilerFor(id, async (tx: string) => {
+      expect(tx).toBe(TX_OLD);
+      // The re-anchor lands between the SELECT and the write: a fresh attempt with its own hash.
+      await owner.query(`UPDATE anchor_store SET status = 'submitting', transaction_hash = $2, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = $1`, [id, TX_NEW]);
+      return { confirmed: true, polled: 1, blockNumber: 99, slot: 990, blockHash: 'bh-old' };
+    });
+    expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 0 });
+    const after = (await owner.query('SELECT status, transaction_hash, block_number FROM anchor_store WHERE id = $1', [id])).rows[0];
+    expect(after).toEqual({ status: 'submitting', transaction_hash: TX_NEW, block_number: null });
+  });
+
+  it('CONTROL: with no move, the same pass confirms the row (the write path is live)', async () => {
+    const id = await insertRow('submitted', TX_OLD, '2 hours');
+    const run = reconcilerFor(id, async () => ({ confirmed: true, polled: 1, blockNumber: 99, slot: 990, blockHash: 'bh-old' }));
+    expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 1, failed: 0 });
+    const after = (await owner.query('SELECT status, transaction_hash, block_number FROM anchor_store WHERE id = $1', [id])).rows[0];
+    expect(after).toEqual({ status: 'confirmed', transaction_hash: TX_OLD, block_number: '99' });
+  });
+});
diff --git a/Blockchain/Dev/services/anchoring/src/__tests__/ks1384-fa-row-count-builder.test.ts b/Blockchain/Dev/services/anchoring/src/__tests__/ks1384-fa-row-count-builder.test.ts
new file mode 100644

+/**
+ * KS-1384 F-a — the row-count builder, its runner, and payloadTimestamp's reader.
+ *
+ * Cells (Seat W 10th's matrix):
+ *   (b1) `expectStatus` (one value or a list) and `expectTransactionHash` (a hash, or null for "no hash")
+ *        narrow the UPDATE's WHERE, with their binds in order. A null hash is `IS NULL`, never `= NULL`
+ *        (which matches no row). Mutants: drop the expectStatus clause; `= $n` for a null hash.
+ *   (c1) `runAnchorStatusUpdate` resolves to the driver's rowCount, and to null when the write did not run or
+ *        its result is unknown (DB unavailable, nothing to write, a throw, no numeric rowCount). The pin's
+ *        version resolved to nothing in every case, so a caller could not tell "the row moved" (0) from "the
+ *        write failed". Mutants: return 0 from the catch; return undefined.
+ *   (pt) `payloadTimestampOf` reads metadata_payload.secuura.timestamp and nothing else. Mutant: read a
+ *        created-at field instead.
+ *   (wu) `buildFlatAnchorMetadataPayload` stamps the `now` it is given, so the route's one Date is the
+ *        anchored timestamp (the deterministic half of cell w; the real-route half is in the integration
+ *        file). Mutant: ignore `now` and call `new Date()` inside the builder.
+ *
+ * The real-Postgres half of (b1), (b2), lives in ks1384-fa-one-date-per-request.integration.test.ts.
+ */
+import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
+import { buildAnchorStatusUpdate, runAnchorStatusUpdate, type RunStatusUpdateDeps } from '../anchorStatusUpdate';
+import { payloadTimestampOf, buildFlatAnchorMetadataPayload, anchorDocumentSchema } from '../anchorSchema';
+
+const NOW = new Date('2026-10-30T02:00:00.000Z');
+const TX = 'a'.repeat(64);
+
+describe('(b1) KS-1384 F-a: expectStatus and expectTransactionHash narrow the WHERE', () => {
+  it('an id-only write is unchanged: WHERE names the id alone and the write is not conditional', () => {
+    const built = buildAnchorStatusUpdate('anchor_1', { status: 'failed' }, NOW)!;
+    expect(built.sql).toBe('UPDATE anchor_store SET status = $1, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $3');
+    expect(built.params).toEqual(['failed', NOW, 'anchor_1']);
+    expect(built.guarded).toBe(false);
+    expect(built.conditional).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it('one expected status adds `status = $n` with its bind after the id', () => {
+    const built = buildAnchorStatusUpdate('anchor_1', { status: 'failed', expectStatus: 'submitted' }, NOW)!;
+    expect(built.sql).toBe('UPDATE anchor_store SET status = $1, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $3 AND status = $4');
+    expect(built.params).toEqual(['failed', NOW, 'anchor_1', 'submitted']);
+    expect(built.conditional).toBe(true);
+    expect(built.guarded).toBe(false);
+  });
+
+  it('a list of expected statuses adds `status = ANY($n::text[])` with the list as one bind', () => {
+    const built = buildAnchorStatusUpdate('anchor_1', { status: 'confirmed', expectStatus: ['pending', 'submitted', 'submitting'] }, NOW)!;
+    expect(built.sql).toBe('UPDATE anchor_store SET status = $1, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $3 AND status = ANY($4::text[])');
+    expect(built.params).toEqual(['confirmed', NOW, 'anchor_1', ['pending', 'submitted', 'submitting']]);
+  });
+
+  it('an expected hash adds `transaction_hash = $n`', () => {
+    const built = buildAnchorStatusUpdate('anchor_1', { status: 'failed', expectTransactionHash: TX }, NOW)!;
+    expect(built.sql).toBe('UPDATE anchor_store SET status = $1, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $3 AND transaction_hash = $4');
+    expect(built.params).toEqual(['failed', NOW, 'anchor_1', TX]);
+  });
+
+  it('an expected NULL hash is `transaction_hash IS NULL` with no bind (never `= NULL`, which matches nothing)', () => {
+    const built = buildAnchorStatusUpdate('anchor_1', { status: 'failed', expectTransactionHash: null }, NOW)!;
+    expect(built.sql).toBe('UPDATE anchor_store SET status = $1, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $3 AND transaction_hash IS NULL');
+    expect(built.params).toEqual(['failed', NOW, 'anchor_1']);
+    expect(built.sql).not.toMatch(/transaction_hash = \$\d+$/);
+  });
+
+  it('all three expectations together keep their binds in order: payload, then status, then hash', () => {
+    const payload = { secuura: { hash: 'h' } };
+    const built = buildAnchorStatusUpdate('anchor_1', {
+      status: 'confirmed',
+      transactionHash: TX,
+      expectMetadataPayload: payload,
+      expectStatus: ['submitting', 'submitted'],
+      expectTransactionHash: TX,
+    }, NOW)!;
+    expect(built.sql).toBe(
+      'UPDATE anchor_store SET status = $1, transaction_hash = $2, simulated = $3, updated_at = $4 ' +
+      'WHERE id = $5 AND metadata_payload = $6::jsonb AND status = ANY($7::text[]) AND transaction_hash = $8',
+    );
+    expect(built.params).toEqual(['confirmed', TX, false, NOW, 'anchor_1', JSON.stringify(payload), ['submitting', 'submitted'], TX]);
+    expect(built.guarded).toBe(true);
+    expect(built.conditional).toBe(true);
+  });
+});
+
+function deps(over: Partial<RunStatusUpdateDeps> = {}) {
+  const log = vi.fn();
+  const d: RunStatusUpdateDeps = {
+    isDbAvailable: () => true,
+    ensureTables: vi.fn(async () => undefined),
+    query: vi.fn(async () => ({ rowCount: 1 })),
+    log,
+    ...over,
+  };
+  return { d, log };
+}
+
+describe('(c1) KS-1384 F-a: runAnchorStatusUpdate says how many rows the write matched, or null', () => {
+  it('a write that matched one row resolves to 1', async () => {
+    const { d } = deps();
+    await expect(runAnchorStatusUpdate(d, 'anchor_1', { status: 'failed' }, NOW)).resolves.toBe(1);
+  });
+
+  it('a write that matched no row resolves to 0, not null', async () => {
+    const { d } = deps({ query: vi.fn(async () => ({ rowCount: 0 })) });
+    await expect(runAnchorStatusUpdate(d, 'anchor_1', { status: 'failed', expectStatus: 'submitted' }, NOW)).resolves.toBe(0);
+  });
+
+  it('a statement that throws resolves to null and logs at warn (still never rethrown)', async () => {
+    const { d, log } = deps({ query: vi.fn(async () => { throw new Error('connection reset'); }) });
+    await expect(runAnchorStatusUpdate(d, 'anchor_1', { status: 'failed' }, NOW)).resolves.toBeNull();
+    expect(log).toHaveBeenCalledWith('warn', 'Failed to update anchor status in DB', { anchorId: 'anchor_1', error: 'connection reset' });
+  });
+
+  it('an unavailable database resolves to null and issues no statement', async () => {
+    const { d } = deps({ isDbAvailable: () => false });
+    await expect(runAnchorStatusUpdate(d, 'anchor_1', { status: 'failed' }, NOW)).resolves.toBeNull();
+    expect(d.query).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+
+  it('nothing to write resolves to null and issues no statement', async () => {
+    const { d } = deps();
+    await expect(runAnchorStatusUpdate(d, 'anchor_1', {}, NOW)).resolves.toBeNull();
+    expect(d.query).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+
+  it('a driver result with no numeric rowCount resolves to null (unknown), never to a count', async () => {
+    const { d } = deps({ query: vi.fn(async () => ({ rows: [] })) });
+    await expect(runAnchorStatusUpdate(d, 'anchor_1', { status: 'failed' }, NOW)).resolves.toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('the statement it runs is the builder\'s, binds included', async () => {
+    const { d } = deps();
+    await runAnchorStatusUpdate(d, 'anchor_1', { status: 'failed', expectTransactionHash: TX }, NOW);
+    const built = buildAnchorStatusUpdate('anchor_1', { status: 'failed', expectTransactionHash: TX }, NOW)!;
+    expect(d.query).toHaveBeenCalledWith(built.sql, built.params);
+  });
+
+  it('KS-1385\'s warn line still fires when a payload-guarded write matches 0, and not for a status-guarded 0', async () => {
+    const { d, log } = deps({ query: vi.fn(async () => ({ rowCount: 0 })) });
+    await runAnchorStatusUpdate(d, 'anchor_1', { status: 'submitted', transactionHash: TX, expectMetadataPayload: { secuura: {} } }, NOW);
+    expect(log).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
+    expect(log.mock.calls[0][1]).toMatch(/^KS-1385: declined to record a transaction/);
+    log.mockClear();
+    await runAnchorStatusUpdate(d, 'anchor_1', { status: 'failed', expectStatus: 'submitted' }, NOW);
+    expect(log).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
+  });
+});
+
+describe('(pt) KS-1384 F-a: payloadTimestampOf reads metadata_payload.secuura.timestamp', () => {
+  it('returns the stored anchoring timestamp', () => {
+    expect(payloadTimestampOf({ secuura: { timestamp: '2026-10-29T08:56:14.000Z', createdAt: 'not-this' } })).toBe('2026-10-29T08:56:14.000Z');
+  });
+
+  it('returns null for a payload with no timestamp, a non-object secuura, or no payload', () => {
+    expect(payloadTimestampOf({ secuura: { hash: 'h' } })).toBeNull();
+    expect(payloadTimestampOf({ secuura: 'scalar' })).toBeNull();
+    expect(payloadTimestampOf({ timestamp: '2026-10-29T08:56:14.000Z' })).toBeNull();
+    expect(payloadTimestampOf(null)).toBeNull();
+    expect(payloadTimestampOf(undefined)).toBeNull();
+  });
+
+  it('a non-string timestamp is not reported', () => {
+    expect(payloadTimestampOf({ secuura: { timestamp: 1730000000000 } })).toBeNull();
+  });
+});
+
+describe('(wu) KS-1384 F-a: the flat payload builder stamps the Date it is given', () => {
+  it('timestamp is exactly the passed instant, not the builder\'s own clock', () => {
+    const data = anchorDocumentSchema.parse({ documentId: 'doc-1730000000000-ks1384wu', contentHash: 'f'.repeat(64) });
+    const fixed = new Date('2026-01-02T03:04:05.678Z');
+    expect(buildFlatAnchorMetadataPayload(data, 'preview', fixed).secuura.timestamp).toBe('2026-01-02T03:04:05.678Z');
+  });
+});

+/**
+ * KS-1384 F-a (design M5): the anchoring time K stamped into the payload a row will anchor or has anchored,
+ * `metadata_payload.secuura.timestamp`, the same value that goes on chain. No column: it is read from the
+ * stored payload. Originate's later resolver uses it to tell a document's own create from a later event that
+ * shares the row (one row per document until per-event anchors). null when the payload carries none.
+ */
+export function payloadTimestampOf(metadataPayload: unknown): string | null {
+  const sec = (metadataPayload as { secuura?: unknown } | null | undefined)?.secuura;
+  if (!sec || typeof sec !== 'object') return null;
+  const ts = (sec as Record<string, unknown>).timestamp;
+  return typeof ts === 'string' ? ts : null;
+}

+
+export interface RunStatusUpdateDeps {
+  isDbAvailable: () => boolean;
+  ensureTables: () => Promise<unknown>;
+  query: (sql: string, params?: unknown[]) => Promise<unknown>;
+  log: (level: 'info' | 'warn' | 'error', message: string, meta?: Record<string, unknown>) => void;
+}
+
+/**
+ * KS-1384 F-a — execute one status UPDATE and say how many rows it matched.
+ *
+ * This is the body `dbUpdateAnchorStatus` (index.ts) used to inline, moved here
+ * so a cell can reach it without booting the service. The pin's version
+ * returned nothing and swallowed every error, so a caller could not tell "the
+ * row moved under me" (0 rows) from "the write never ran" (a database fault).
+ *
+ * Returns:
+ *   - the matched row count (0 or 1 for an id-keyed UPDATE);
+ *   - `null` when the write did not run or its result is unknown: the database
+ *     is unavailable, there was nothing to write, the statement threw, or the
+ *     driver reported no numeric `rowCount`.
+ * A caller must treat `null` as "unknown", never as success. The errors are
+ * still logged at warn and still not rethrown: every caller at this pin ignores
+ * the result, and KS-1384 F0 is the first that reads it.
+ */
+export async function runAnchorStatusUpdate(
+  deps: RunStatusUpdateDeps,
+  anchorId: string,
+  updates: AnchorStatusUpdate,
+  now: Date = new Date(),
+): Promise<number | null> {
+  try {
+    if (!deps.isDbAvailable()) return null;
+    await deps.ensureTables();
+
+    const built = buildAnchorStatusUpdate(anchorId, updates, now);
+    if (!built) return null;
+
+    const result: any = await deps.query(built.sql, built.params);
+    const affected = typeof result?.rowCount === 'number' ? result.rowCount : null;
+
+    // KS-1385 MIN-1: a guarded write that matched NO row is not an error — it
+    // is the race being caught. Say so once, at warn, because an operator
+    // seeing a paid transaction with no row to show for it needs this line to
+    // explain it. The transaction is on chain and honest; it just is not the
+    // one this row indexes any more (KS-1384 is the structural ticket for a
+    // row that can only index one).
+    if (built.guarded && affected === 0) {
+      deps.log('warn', 'KS-1385: declined to record a transaction against an anchor whose metadata changed under it — a later event had already replaced the payload', {
+        anchorId,
+        status: updates.status,
+        transactionHash: updates.transactionHash,
+      });
+    }
+    return affected;
+  } catch (err: any) {
+    deps.log('warn', 'Failed to update anchor status in DB', { anchorId, error: err?.message });
+    return null;
+  }
+}

+ *     quota error) never fails a row and resets the count, because "could not
+ *     ask" says nothing about the transaction;
+ *   - KS-1384 F-a: each write expects the status set and the hash this pass
+ *     read, so a row that moved between the SELECT and the write (a new hash
+ *     from a re-anchor, or a terminal state written by the submitter) is left
+ *     as it is. Only a write that matched its row is counted.
  *
  * Dependency-injected so it is unit-testable without booting the service
  * (index.ts starts the HTTP listener on import).
  * =============================================================================
  */
 
+export interface ReconcilerCheck {
+  confirmed: boolean;
+  blockHash?: string;
+  blockNumber?: number;
+  slot?: number;
+  confirmations?: number;
+  /** KS-726: attempts in which the chain answered (found or "not found"). */
+  polled?: number;
+  /** KS-726: attempts that threw before the chain answered. */
+  errored?: number;
+}

+/**
+ * KS-1384 F-a (design §4.1, D13) — originate's two anchor-create routes no longer write anchor_store.
+ *
+ * WHAT WAS WRONG. `saveAnchorToDb` (routes/anchors.ts) upserted anchor_store on (document_id, network) after
+ * forwarding to anchoring. It wrote the anchoring service's ANCHOR ID into `transaction_hash`, a
+ * `confirmed_at` of "now", and `updated_at = NOW()`, over the row anchoring owns and was still processing.
+ * F-a moves the reconciler's 24 h give-up from `created_at` to `updated_at` (KS-1808); a second writer that
+ * bumps `updated_at` from outside would keep such a row from ever being given up, so the write goes with it.
+ *

+ * after reaching anchoring, and call `prisma.$executeRaw` zero times. The recorder seeing exactly the one
+ * forwarded request is the control that the route ran far enough for the old write to have happened. The
+ * non-production "anchoring unreachable" 202 no longer claims the request was persisted locally.

+<h2>125. Anchor status writes say how many rows they matched, the flat anchor create answers the anchoring time it will put on chain, originate no longer writes the anchoring service&rsquo;s table, and the reconciler fails a row only after 24 h without a write and two answered &ldquo;not found&rdquo; passes (KS-1384 F-a)</h2>
+<table class="pmatrix"><tr><td class="pm-topic" rowspan="6"><strong>What this changes.</strong></td><td><strong>The row-count builder.</strong> <code>services/anchoring/src/anchorStatusUpdate.ts</code>: the status UPDATE builder gains <code>expectStatus</code> (one value, or a list read as <code>status = ANY(...)</code>) and <code>expectTransactionHash</code> (<code>null</code> means <code>IS NULL</code>, never <code>= NULL</code>). The body of <code>dbUpdateAnchorStatus</code> moves into an exported <code>runAnchorStatusUpdate</code>, which resolves to the matched row count, or to <code>null</code> when the write did not run or its result is unknown. The reconciler (below) reads the count; the submitter still ignores it (KS-1384 F0 is the first there).</td></tr><tr><td><strong>One Date per request.</strong> <code>POST /api/anchors</code> takes one <code>Date</code> and uses it for the payload&rsquo;s anchored <code>timestamp</code> and the row&rsquo;s <code>created_at</code>. The 202 gains <code>payloadTimestamp</code>: the timestamp inside the payload the row holds after the upsert, read in the upsert&rsquo;s own <code>RETURNING</code>, so a repeat request whose KS-1385 overwrite was declined reports the kept payload&rsquo;s time. <code>GET /api/anchors/:id</code> gains the same field from the stored payload (null when it carries none). The published spec adds the field to the 202 schema (additive).</td></tr><tr><td><strong>originate stops writing <code>anchor_store</code>.</strong> <code>saveAnchorToDb</code> in <code>services/originate/src/routes/anchors.ts</code> is removed with its two calls. It wrote the anchoring service&rsquo;s anchor id into <code>transaction_hash</code>, a <code>confirmed_at</code> of now, and <code>updated_at = NOW()</code> over the row anchoring owns. The non-production &ldquo;anchoring unreachable&rdquo; 202 no longer says the request was persisted locally.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>The reconciler&rsquo;s give-up (KS-1808, KS-1810).</strong> <code>services/anchoring/src/reconciler.ts</code>: the 24 h window runs from the row&rsquo;s last write (<code>updated_at</code>), not <code>created_at</code>, so a re-armed old row is no longer failed at its first pass. A row is failed only after TWO consecutive passes past the window in which the chain ANSWERED &ldquo;not found&rdquo; (the <code>polled</code> count of <code>waitForConfirmation</code>); a pass the provider could not answer never fails a row and starts the count again, as does a thrown check or a new hash on the row. The count is in memory, keyed by anchor id and hash, so a restart can only delay a failed write.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Guarded reconciler writes.</strong> Each confirmed or failed write expects the status set the pass selected (<code>pending</code>, <code>submitted</code>, <code>submitting</code>) and the hash it read, and only a write that matched its row (1) is counted; 0 (the row moved: a re-anchor or a final status) and <code>null</code> (the write did not run) are logged and left.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Comments and two pins.</strong> The reconciler header now states the new rule; its MIN_AGE comment (KS-1816) is kept, because it is still true; its catch comment says a provider outage is decided by the &ldquo;could not ask&rdquo; branch. <code>ks1816-reconciler-comments-match-the-code.test.ts</code> is rewritten in the same commit: R1 pins the new header sentence, C2 pins the <code>updated_at</code> give-up line; R2, C1 and C3 are unchanged. A comment in <code>dbPersistAnchor</code> named its two callers in call form, which the KS-1385 W1 source pin counted as two more calls (78 of 79 green); it now names them in words.</td></tr></table>
+<h3>125.1 What proves it</h3>
+<table class="pmatrix"><tr><td class="pm-topic" rowspan="7"><strong>Red at the base, green after, every mutant killed by its own cell.</strong></td><td><strong>Unit, part 1</strong> (<code>ks1384-fa-row-count-builder.test.ts</code>, 18 cells: (b1) the WHERE clauses, (c1) count or null, (pt) the payload reader, (wu) the builder stamps the Date it is given). Against develop&rsquo;s modules: 18 of 18 red. Real-copy control: green.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Unit, the reconciler</strong> (<code>ks1384-fa-reconciler-give-up-and-answered-passes.test.ts</code>, 13 cells: (r) the window runs from the last write, (t) only answered passes count and the count restarts, (r3s) every write expects what it read and only a 1 is counted). Against develop&rsquo;s <code>reconciler.ts</code>: 13 of 13 red. Real-copy control: green. The existing <code>ks584-reconciler.test.ts</code> &ldquo;fails a real tx absent past the window&rdquo; cell now runs two passes and checks the guard (6 of 6 green).</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Real Postgres</strong> (<code>ks1384-fa-one-date-per-request.integration.test.ts</code>, 3 cells: (b2) the expectations executed, (w) 202 <code>payloadTimestamp</code> = stored timestamp = <code>created_at</code>, (w2) a declined overwrite reports the kept time; against develop: 3 of 3 red. <code>ks1384-fa-reconciler-writes.integration.test.ts</code>, 3 cells: (r2) a not-yet pass writes nothing, (r3) a row re-anchored during the chain check is not confirmed over, and a control; against develop&rsquo;s reconciler: (r3) red, (r2) and the control green, (r2) red by its mutant). Real-copy controls: green.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>originate</strong> (<code>ks1384-fa-anchor-routes-write-no-anchor-row.test.ts</code>, 3 cells (sa)): both routes reach anchoring and call <code>prisma.$executeRaw</code> zero times. Against develop&rsquo;s route: 3 of 3 red. Real-copy control: green.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Mutants</strong>, each parsed before it ran and each killed only by its own cell: (b1) drop the <code>expectStatus</code> clause, 3 red; (b1) <code>= $n</code> for a null hash, 1 red; (c1) the catch returns 0, 1 red; (c1) resolve to undefined, 3 red; (pt) read a created-at field, 1 red; (wu) the builder ignores the Date it is given, 1 red; (b2) <code>= NULL</code> on Postgres, 1 red; (w) a distinct instant for the payload, 1 red; (w2) answer the incoming timestamp, 1 red; (sa) a write put back in <code>/submit</code>, 1 red; (sa) the old message, 1 red; (r) read <code>created_at</code>, 1 red; (r2) touch the row inside the window, 1 red; (r3) drop <code>expectTransactionHash</code> on Postgres, 1 red; (r3s) drop <code>expectTransactionHash</code>, 2 red; (r3s) drop <code>expectStatus</code>, 2 red; (r3s) count whatever the write returned, 1 red; (t) treat every not-confirmed pass as answered, 2 red; (t) fail on the first answered pass, 6 red (five (t) cells and the (r) cell that asserts the second pass, the same rule); (t) count by anchor id alone, 1 red; (t) no reset on an errored pass, 1 red; (t) no reset on a thrown check, 1 red.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>The KS-1816 pin, both ways</strong> (each run on a scratch copy): the rewritten pin is 5 of 5 green on this <code>reconciler.ts</code> and 2 of 5 red (R1, C2) on develop&rsquo;s; the pin as develop has it is 2 of 5 red (R1, C2) on this file and 5 of 5 green on develop&rsquo;s.</td></tr><tr><td><strong>Two existing files updated by part 1:</strong> <code>ks1822-a-foreign-tenants-anchor-reads-as-absent.test.ts</code> deep-equals the whole GET body, so its expected body gains <code>payloadTimestamp: null</code> (6 of 12 red without it); <code>ks1293-originate-suite-is-hermetic.test.ts</code> lists the new originate file in its manifest (MANIFEST-DRIFT red without it).</td></tr></table>

+<h3>125.3 What this does not prove</h3>
+<table class="pmatrix"><tr><td class="pm-topic" rowspan="4"><strong>Stated, not claimed.</strong></td><td>That the old two-<code>new Date()</code> shape ever produced different instants on a box: it differs only when the two calls straddle a millisecond, which a real-route cell cannot force. Cell (wu) pins the deterministic half and cell (w)&rsquo;s mutant passes a distinct instant.</td></tr><tr><td>How a real provider behaves in an outage: the cells feed <code>polled</code> and <code>errored</code> counts; that the production check reports <code>polled: 0</code> when every attempt threw is read from <code>cardano/confirmation.ts</code>, not measured against Blockfrost.</td></tr><tr><td>That two answered passes are enough on a congested chain: the count is the design&rsquo;s choice, and a restart resets it (it can only delay a failed write).</td></tr><tr><td>The four platform suites, a running stack, Node 24 (LTS), and any box: not run here.</td></tr></table>

+<h2>KS-1384 F-a: row counts, payloadTimestamp, originate stops writing anchor_store, and the reconciler&rsquo;s give-up</h2>
+<div class="note"><table class="pmatrix"><tr><td class="pm-topic" rowspan="4"><strong>What to know.</strong></td><td>Anchoring&rsquo;s status UPDATE can expect a status (or a list) and a hash (or no hash), and the runner says how many rows matched, or <code>null</code> when the write did not run. The reconciler reads it; the submitter does not yet.</td></tr><tr><td><code>POST /api/anchors</code> answers <code>payloadTimestamp</code> (the stored payload&rsquo;s anchoring time; the kept payload&rsquo;s time when a repeat request did not replace it), and <code>GET /api/anchors/:id</code> answers it too. Additive.</td></tr><tr><td>originate&rsquo;s <code>POST /api/anchoring/anchor</code> and <code>/submit</code> no longer write <code>anchor_store</code>; anchoring persists its own row.</td></tr><tr><td>The reconciler fails a row only 24 h after its last write and after two consecutive passes in which the chain answered &ldquo;not found&rdquo;; a provider outage never fails a row. Each of its writes expects the status and hash it read.</td></tr></table></div>
+<div class="note"><table class="pmatrix"><tr><td class="pm-topic" rowspan="1"><strong>Not measured here.</strong></td><td>A real provider outage, the four platform suites, a running stack, Node 24 (LTS), any box.</td></tr></table></div>

### FILE AT HEAD: Blockchain/Dev/services/anchoring/src/__tests__/ks1384-fa-one-date-per-request.integration.test.ts
1: /**
2:  * KS-1384 F-a — real-Postgres cells for the row-count builder and payloadTimestamp (Seat W 10th's matrix).
3:  *
4:  *   (b2) The builder's expectations, EXECUTED: a row with no hash matches `expectTransactionHash: null`
5:  *        (`IS NULL`); a row whose status is not the expected one matches 0 and is left as it was.
6:  *        Mutant: `transaction_hash = NULL` for the null case (never matches).
7:  *   (w)  One `Date` per request (design M5): for a fresh row, the 202's payloadTimestamp, the stored
8:  *        payload's `secuura.timestamp` and the row's created_at are the same instant.
9:  *        Mutant: two `new Date()` calls (the payload's timestamp and the row's created_at taken apart).
10:  *   (w2) A repeat request for the same flat document id, whose KS-1385 overwrite is declined (a non-uuid id
11:  *        that names no document keeps the stored payload, by design), answers payloadTimestamp = the KEPT
12:  *        payload's time, read in the upsert's own RETURNING, not this request's.
13:  *        Mutant: answer the incoming payload's timestamp.
14:  *
15:  * Needs a Postgres carrying the repo schema: TEST_DATABASE_URL. It FAILS when that is unset — it never skips
16:  * (workspace no-skip rule). No chain keys: anchoring runs its mock path, which never touches a network.
17:  */

26: const OWNER_URL = process.env.TEST_DATABASE_URL;

29: const ORG = '1a138400-0000-4000-8000-0000000138a1';
30: const USER = '2a138400-0000-4000-8000-0000000138a2';
31: const HASH = 'd'.repeat(64);
32: 
33: const { privateKey, publicKey } = crypto.generateKeyPairSync('rsa', { modulusLength: 2048 });
34: const PRIVATE_PEM = privateKey.export({ type: 'pkcs8', format: 'pem' }).toString();
35: const PUBLIC_PEM = publicKey.export({ type: 'spki', format: 'pem' }).toString();
36: const b64url = (b: Buffer): string => b.toString('base64').replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '');
37: function token(): string {
38:   const header = b64url(Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ alg: 'RS256', typ: 'JWT' })));
39:   const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
40:   const payload = b64url(Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ iat: now, exp: now + 600, role: 'user', verificationLevel: 'STANDARD', userId: USER, organizationId: ORG, tenantId: TENANT })));
41:   return `${header}.${payload}.${b64url(crypto.sign('RSA-SHA256', Buffer.from(`${header}.${payload}`), PRIVATE_PEM))}`;
42: }
43: 
44: let server: http.Server;
45: let base: string;
46: let owner: Client;
47: const madeDocIds: string[] = [];
48: const madeAnchorIds: string[] = [];
49: 
50: let seq = 0;
51: function flatId(): string { seq += 1; return `doc-${Date.now()}${seq}-ks1384fa${seq}`; }
52: 
53: async function postAnchor(documentId: string) {
54:   const res = await fetch(`${base}/api/anchors`, {
55:     method: 'POST',
56:     headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Authorization: `Bearer ${token()}` },
57:     body: JSON.stringify({ documentId, contentHash: HASH }),
58:   });
59:   const json = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as { data?: { id?: string; payloadTimestamp?: string | null; createdAt?: string } };
60:   if (json?.data?.id) madeAnchorIds.push(json.data.id);
61:   return { status: res.status, json };
62: }
63: 
64: async function storedRow(documentId: string) {
65:   const r = await owner.query(
66:     `SELECT id, created_at, metadata_payload->'secuura'->>'timestamp' AS payload_ts FROM anchor_store WHERE document_id = $1`,
67:     [documentId],
68:   );
69:   return r.rows;
70: }

110: describe('(b2) KS-1384 F-a: the builder\'s expectations, executed on Postgres', () => {
111:   it('a row with no hash matches expectTransactionHash: null; a status that moved matches 0 and the row is untouched', async () => {
112:     const id = `anchor_${crypto.randomUUID()}`;

137:   });

160:     expect(typeof firstTs).toBe('string');
161: 
162:     await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 25)); // the second request's own clock is strictly later
163:     const second = await postAnchor(doc);
164:     expect(second.status, JSON.stringify(second.json)).toBe(202);
165: 
166:     // CONTROL: the overwrite really was declined — the row still holds the first payload's timestamp.
167:     const rows = await storedRow(doc);
168:     expect(rows).toHaveLength(1);
169:     expect(rows[0].payload_ts).toBe(firstTs);
170:     // THE CELL: the second 202 reports the kept payload's time, not its own request's.
171:     expect(second.json.data?.payloadTimestamp).toBe(firstTs);
172:     // And the request's own clock did move on: its createdAt (this request's Date) is later than firstTs.
173:     expect(Date.parse(String(second.json.data?.createdAt))).toBeGreaterThan(Date.parse(firstTs));
174:   });
175: });

### FILE AT HEAD: Blockchain/Dev/services/anchoring/src/__tests__/ks1384-fa-reconciler-give-up-and-answered-passes.test.ts
1: /**
2:  * KS-1384 F-a — the reconciler's give-up (Seat W 10th's matrix, unit half; the real-Postgres half is
3:  * ks1384-fa-reconciler-writes.integration.test.ts).
4:  *
5:  *   (r)   KS-1808: the 24 h window runs from the row's LAST WRITE (`updated_at`). A row created 30 days ago and
6:  *         re-armed now is not failed; a row silent for 25 h is, after two answered passes.
7:  *         Mutant: read `created_at`.
8:  *   (t)   KS-1810: a pass in which the chain could not be asked (`polled 0, errored 1`) never fails a row; two
9:  *         CONSECUTIVE answered "not found" passes past the window do; an errored pass between them, a thrown
10:  *         check, or a new hash on the row starts the count again.
11:  *         Mutants: read `confirmed` only (treat every not-confirmed as answered); no reset on an errored pass;
12:  *         count by anchor id alone; fail on the first answered pass.
13:  *   (r3s) Every reconciler write expects the status set and the hash it read, and only a write that matched
14:  *         its row (1) is counted: 0 (the row moved) and null (the write did not run) are not.
15:  *         Mutants: drop `expectTransactionHash`; drop `expectStatus`; count whatever the write returned.
16:  */
17: import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
18: import { createStuckAnchorReconciler, ANSWERED_MISSES_TO_FAIL, type ReconcilerDeps, type ReconcilerCheck } from '../reconciler';
19: 
20: const TX_A = 'a1'.repeat(32);
21: const TX_B = 'b2'.repeat(32);
22: const HOUR = 3_600_000;
23: const NOW = Date.parse('2026-10-30T02:00:00Z');
24: const CONFIG = { minAgeMs: 10 * 60_000, giveUpMs: 24 * HOUR, batch: 25 };
25: 
26: const ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND: ReconcilerCheck = { confirmed: false, polled: 1, errored: 0 };
27: const PROVIDER_ERROR: ReconcilerCheck = { confirmed: false, polled: 0, errored: 1 };
28: 
29: function row(over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
30:   return { id: 'anchor-fa', status: 'submitted', transaction_hash: TX_A, created_at: new Date(NOW - 2 * HOUR).toISOString(), updated_at: new Date(NOW - 2 * HOUR).toISOString(), ...over };
31: }
32: 
33: /** A reconciler whose rows and chain answers are scripted per pass. */
34: function harness(passes: Array<{ rows: any[]; check: ReconcilerCheck | (() => never) }>, writeResult: number | null = 1) {
35:   const writes: Array<{ id: string; updates: Record<string, unknown> }> = [];
36:   const log = vi.fn();
37:   let pass = -1;
38:   const deps: ReconcilerDeps = {
39:     isDbAvailable: () => true,
40:     isCardanoReady: () => true,
41:     query: vi.fn(async () => { pass += 1; return { rows: passes[pass].rows }; }),
42:     checkTx: vi.fn(async () => {
43:       const c = passes[pass].check;
44:       if (typeof c === 'function') return c();
45:       return c;
46:     }),
47:     updateStatus: vi.fn(async (id: string, updates: Record<string, unknown>) => { writes.push({ id, updates }); return writeResult; }),
48:     log,
49:     now: () => NOW,
50:   };
51:   const run = createStuckAnchorReconciler(deps, CONFIG);
52:   return { run, writes, log };
53: }
54: 
55: describe('(r) KS-1808: the give-up window runs from the last write', () => {
56:   it('a row created 30 days ago and re-armed 11 minutes ago is not failed, however many answered passes', async () => {
57:     const reArmed = row({ created_at: new Date(NOW - 30 * 24 * HOUR).toISOString(), updated_at: new Date(NOW - 11 * 60_000).toISOString() });
58:     const { run, writes } = harness([0, 1, 2].map(() => ({ rows: [reArmed], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND })));
59:     for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 0 });
60:     expect(writes).toHaveLength(0);
61:   });
62: 
63:   it('a row silent for 25 hours is failed at its second answered pass', async () => {
64:     const silent = row({ created_at: new Date(NOW - 25 * HOUR).toISOString(), updated_at: new Date(NOW - 25 * HOUR).toISOString() });
65:     const { run, writes } = harness([0, 1].map(() => ({ rows: [silent], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND })));
66:     expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 0 });
67:     expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 1 });
68:     expect(writes).toHaveLength(1);
69:     expect(writes[0].updates.status).toBe('failed');
70:   });
71: });
72: 
73: // Old on BOTH clocks, so the pin's created_at reconciler would also give it up: the (t) cells then separate the
74: // two rules on the answered count alone.
75: const PAST = () => row({ created_at: new Date(NOW - 25 * HOUR).toISOString(), updated_at: new Date(NOW - 25 * HOUR).toISOString() });
76: 
77: describe('(t) KS-1810: only answered "not found" passes count, and they must be consecutive', () => {
78:   it('the rule needs two answered passes (the exported constant the cells below rely on)', () => {
79:     expect(ANSWERED_MISSES_TO_FAIL).toBe(2);
80:   });
81: 
82:   it('a provider that never answers never fails a row past the window, and nothing is written', async () => {
83:     const { run, writes, log } = harness([0, 1, 2, 3].map(() => ({ rows: [PAST()], check: PROVIDER_ERROR })));
84:     for (let i = 0; i < 4; i += 1) expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 0 });
85:     expect(writes).toHaveLength(0);
86:     expect(log.mock.calls.some((c) => /could not be asked/.test(String(c[1])))).toBe(true);
87:   });
88: 
89:   it('two consecutive answered passes fail the row at the second, not the first', async () => {
90:     const { run, writes } = harness([0, 1].map(() => ({ rows: [PAST()], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND })));
91:     expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
92:     expect(writes).toHaveLength(0);
93:     expect((await run()).failed).toBe(1);
94:   });
95: 
96:   it('answered, errored, answered: not failed (the error restarted the count); a fourth answered pass fails it', async () => {
97:     const { run, writes } = harness([ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND, PROVIDER_ERROR, ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND, ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND].map((check) => ({ rows: [PAST()], check })));
98:     expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
99:     expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
100:     expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
101:     expect(writes).toHaveLength(0);
102:     expect((await run()).failed).toBe(1);
103:   });
104: 
105:   it('a thrown check restarts the count too', async () => {
106:     const boom = () => { throw new Error('blockfrost 429'); };
107:     const { run, writes } = harness([
108:       { rows: [PAST()], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND },
109:       { rows: [PAST()], check: boom as () => never },
110:       { rows: [PAST()], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND },
111:     ]);
112:     for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
113:     expect(writes).toHaveLength(0);
114:   });
115: 
116:   it('a new hash on the row starts the count again (the count belongs to the transaction, not the anchor id)', async () => {
117:     const { run, writes } = harness([
118:       { rows: [PAST()], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND },
119:       { rows: [row({ transaction_hash: TX_B, created_at: new Date(NOW - 25 * HOUR).toISOString(), updated_at: new Date(NOW - 25 * HOUR).toISOString() })], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND },
120:     ]);
121:     expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
122:     expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
123:     expect(writes).toHaveLength(0);
124:   });
125: 
126:   it('inside the window an answered pass is not counted: the first answered pass after it crosses is still only the first', async () => {
127:     const young = row({ updated_at: new Date(NOW - 2 * HOUR).toISOString() });
128:     const { run, writes } = harness([
129:       { rows: [young], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND },
130:       { rows: [PAST()], check: ANSWERED_NOT_FOUND },
131:     ]);
132:     expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
133:     expect((await run()).failed).toBe(0);
134:     expect(writes).toHaveLength(0);
135:   });
136: });
137: 
138: describe('(r3s) every reconciler write expects what it read, and only a matched write counts', () => {
139:   it('the confirmed write expects the non-final statuses and the read hash', async () => {
