### FILE AT HEAD: Blockchain/Dev/services/anchoring/src/__tests__/ks1384-fa-reconciler-writes.integration.test.ts
1: /**
2:  * KS-1384 F-a — the reconciler against real Postgres (Seat W 10th's matrix).
3:  *
4:  *   (r2) A pass that finds the transaction not yet on chain, inside the window, writes nothing: the row's
5:  *        updated_at is unchanged (so KS-1808's window, which reads updated_at, is not pushed by the reconciler
6:  *        itself). Mutant: a touch in the not-yet branch.
7:  *   (r3) The reconciler's write misses when the row moved to a new hash between its SELECT and its write (a
8:  *        re-anchor upsert landing during the chain check): the row keeps the new hash and its status, and the
9:  *        pass counts nothing. Mutant: drop `expectTransactionHash` from the reconciler's guard.
10:  *
11:  * The reconciler runs with its real SELECT (deps.query on Postgres) and its writes through the product's
12:  * `runAnchorStatusUpdate`. Only the chain check is scripted. Needs TEST_DATABASE_URL; FAILS when unset.
13:  */
14: import { describe, it, expect, beforeAll, afterAll } from 'vitest';
15: import crypto from 'crypto';
16: import { Client } from 'pg';
17: import { createStuckAnchorReconciler } from '../reconciler';
18: import { runAnchorStatusUpdate } from '../anchorStatusUpdate';
19: 
20: const OWNER_URL = process.env.TEST_DATABASE_URL;
21: const HASH = 'e'.repeat(64);
22: const TX_OLD = 'c3'.repeat(32);
23: const TX_NEW = 'd4'.repeat(32);
24: const made: string[] = [];
25: let owner: Client;
26: 
27: beforeAll(async () => {
28:   if (!OWNER_URL) throw new Error('KS-1384 F-a reconciler integration cells need TEST_DATABASE_URL — refusing to pass without it');
29:   owner = new Client({ connectionString: OWNER_URL });
30:   await owner.connect();
31: });
32: 
33: afterAll(async () => {
34:   if (owner) {
35:     if (made.length) await owner.query('DELETE FROM anchor_store WHERE id = ANY($1::text[])', [made]).catch(() => undefined);
36:     await owner.end();
37:   }
38: });
39: 
40: async function insertRow(status: string, tx: string, updatedAgo: string): Promise<string> {
41:   const id = `anchor_${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
42:   made.push(id);
43:   await owner.query(
44:     `INSERT INTO anchor_store (id, document_id, anchor_type, content_hash, network, metadata_payload, status, transaction_hash, simulated, created_at, updated_at)
45:      VALUES ($1, $2, 'single', $3, 'preview', '{}'::jsonb, $4, $5, false, NOW() - $6::interval, NOW() - $6::interval)`,
46:     [id, `doc-ks1384fa-${id.slice(-12)}`, HASH, status, tx, updatedAgo],
47:   );
48:   return id;
49: }
50: 
51: function reconcilerFor(onlyId: string, check: (tx: string) => Promise<any>) {
52:   const deps = { isDbAvailable: () => true, ensureTables: async () => undefined, query: (s: string, p?: unknown[]) => owner.query(s, p as any[]), log: () => undefined };
53:   return createStuckAnchorReconciler({
54:     isDbAvailable: () => true,
55:     isCardanoReady: () => true,
56:     // The real SELECT, narrowed to this cell's row so rows from other cells or other suites cannot interfere.
57:     query: async (sql: string, params?: unknown[]) => {
58:       const r = await owner.query(sql, params as any[]);
59:       return { rows: r.rows.filter((x: any) => x.id === onlyId) };
60:     },
61:     checkTx: check,
62:     updateStatus: (anchorId: string, updates: Record<string, unknown>) => runAnchorStatusUpdate(deps, anchorId, updates as any),
63:     log: () => undefined,
64:   }, { minAgeMs: 10 * 60_000, giveUpMs: 24 * 3_600_000, batch: 10_000 });
65: }
66: 
67: describe('(r2) KS-1384 F-a: a not-yet pass writes nothing', () => {
68:   it('inside the window, an answered "not found" leaves updated_at exactly as it was', async () => {
69:     const id = await insertRow('submitted', TX_OLD, '2 hours');
70:     const before = (await owner.query('SELECT updated_at, status, error_message FROM anchor_store WHERE id = $1', [id])).rows[0];
71:     const run = reconcilerFor(id, async () => ({ confirmed: false, polled: 1, errored: 0 }));
72:     // CONTROL: the row is selected (scanned 1), so a write would have been possible.
73:     expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 0 });
74:     const after = (await owner.query('SELECT updated_at, status, error_message FROM anchor_store WHERE id = $1', [id])).rows[0];
75:     expect(new Date(after.updated_at).getTime()).toBe(new Date(before.updated_at).getTime());
76:     expect(after.status).toBe('submitted');
77:     expect(after.error_message).toBeNull();
78:   });
79: });
80: 
81: describe('(r3) KS-1384 F-a: the reconciler\'s write misses a row that moved to a new hash', () => {
82:   it('a re-anchor during the chain check: the confirmed write matches 0, the row keeps the new hash and status', async () => {
83:     const id = await insertRow('submitted', TX_OLD, '2 hours');
84:     const run = reconcilerFor(id, async (tx: string) => {
85:       expect(tx).toBe(TX_OLD);
86:       // The re-anchor lands between the SELECT and the write: a fresh attempt with its own hash.
87:       await owner.query(`UPDATE anchor_store SET status = 'submitting', transaction_hash = $2, updated_at = NOW() WHERE id = $1`, [id, TX_NEW]);
88:       return { confirmed: true, polled: 1, blockNumber: 99, slot: 990, blockHash: 'bh-old' };
89:     });
90:     expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 0 });
91:     const after = (await owner.query('SELECT status, transaction_hash, block_number FROM anchor_store WHERE id = $1', [id])).rows[0];
92:     expect(after).toEqual({ status: 'submitting', transaction_hash: TX_NEW, block_number: null });
93:   });
94: 
95:   it('CONTROL: with no move, the same pass confirms the row (the write path is live)', async () => {
96:     const id = await insertRow('submitted', TX_OLD, '2 hours');
97:     const run = reconcilerFor(id, async () => ({ confirmed: true, polled: 1, blockNumber: 99, slot: 990, blockHash: 'bh-old' }));
98:     expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 1, failed: 0 });
99:     const after = (await owner.query('SELECT status, transaction_hash, block_number FROM anchor_store WHERE id = $1', [id])).rows[0];
100:     expect(after).toEqual({ status: 'confirmed', transaction_hash: TX_OLD, block_number: '99' });
101:   });
102: });

### FILE AT HEAD: Blockchain/Dev/services/anchoring/src/__tests__/ks1384-fa-row-count-builder.test.ts
1: /**
2:  * KS-1384 F-a — the row-count builder, its runner, and payloadTimestamp's reader.
3:  *
4:  * Cells (Seat W 10th's matrix):
5:  *   (b1) `expectStatus` (one value or a list) and `expectTransactionHash` (a hash, or null for "no hash")
6:  *        narrow the UPDATE's WHERE, with their binds in order. A null hash is `IS NULL`, never `= NULL`
7:  *        (which matches no row). Mutants: drop the expectStatus clause; `= $n` for a null hash.
8:  *   (c1) `runAnchorStatusUpdate` resolves to the driver's rowCount, and to null when the write did not run or
9:  *        its result is unknown (DB unavailable, nothing to write, a throw, no numeric rowCount). The pin's
10:  *        version resolved to nothing in every case, so a caller could not tell "the row moved" (0) from "the
11:  *        write failed". Mutants: return 0 from the catch; return undefined.
12:  *   (pt) `payloadTimestampOf` reads metadata_payload.secuura.timestamp and nothing else. Mutant: read a
13:  *        created-at field instead.
14:  *   (wu) `buildFlatAnchorMetadataPayload` stamps the `now` it is given, so the route's one Date is the
15:  *        anchored timestamp (the deterministic half of cell w; the real-route half is in the integration
16:  *        file). Mutant: ignore `now` and call `new Date()` inside the builder.
17:  *
18:  * The real-Postgres half of (b1), (b2), lives in ks1384-fa-one-date-per-request.integration.test.ts.
19:  */
20: import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
21: import { buildAnchorStatusUpdate, runAnchorStatusUpdate, type RunStatusUpdateDeps } from '../anchorStatusUpdate';
22: import { payloadTimestampOf, buildFlatAnchorMetadataPayload, anchorDocumentSchema } from '../anchorSchema';
23: 
24: const NOW = new Date('2026-10-30T02:00:00.000Z');
25: const TX = 'a'.repeat(64);
26: 
27: describe('(b1) KS-1384 F-a: expectStatus and expectTransactionHash narrow the WHERE', () => {
28:   it('an id-only write is unchanged: WHERE names the id alone and the write is not conditional', () => {
29:     const built = buildAnchorStatusUpdate('anchor_1', { status: 'failed' }, NOW)!;
30:     expect(built.sql).toBe('UPDATE anchor_store SET status = $1, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $3');
31:     expect(built.params).toEqual(['failed', NOW, 'anchor_1']);
32:     expect(built.guarded).toBe(false);
33:     expect(built.conditional).toBe(false);
34:   });
35: 
36:   it('one expected status adds `status = $n` with its bind after the id', () => {
37:     const built = buildAnchorStatusUpdate('anchor_1', { status: 'failed', expectStatus: 'submitted' }, NOW)!;
38:     expect(built.sql).toBe('UPDATE anchor_store SET status = $1, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $3 AND status = $4');
39:     expect(built.params).toEqual(['failed', NOW, 'anchor_1', 'submitted']);
40:     expect(built.conditional).toBe(true);
41:     expect(built.guarded).toBe(false);
42:   });
43: 
44:   it('a list of expected statuses adds `status = ANY($n::text[])` with the list as one bind', () => {
45:     const built = buildAnchorStatusUpdate('anchor_1', { status: 'confirmed', expectStatus: ['pending', 'submitted', 'submitting'] }, NOW)!;
46:     expect(built.sql).toBe('UPDATE anchor_store SET status = $1, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $3 AND status = ANY($4::text[])');
47:     expect(built.params).toEqual(['confirmed', NOW, 'anchor_1', ['pending', 'submitted', 'submitting']]);
48:   });
49: 
50:   it('an expected hash adds `transaction_hash = $n`', () => {
51:     const built = buildAnchorStatusUpdate('anchor_1', { status: 'failed', expectTransactionHash: TX }, NOW)!;
52:     expect(built.sql).toBe('UPDATE anchor_store SET status = $1, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $3 AND transaction_hash = $4');
53:     expect(built.params).toEqual(['failed', NOW, 'anchor_1', TX]);
54:   });
55: 
56:   it('an expected NULL hash is `transaction_hash IS NULL` with no bind (never `= NULL`, which matches nothing)', () => {
57:     const built = buildAnchorStatusUpdate('anchor_1', { status: 'failed', expectTransactionHash: null }, NOW)!;
58:     expect(built.sql).toBe('UPDATE anchor_store SET status = $1, updated_at = $2 WHERE id = $3 AND transaction_hash IS NULL');
59:     expect(built.params).toEqual(['failed', NOW, 'anchor_1']);
60:     expect(built.sql).not.toMatch(/transaction_hash = \$\d+$/);
61:   });
62: 
63:   it('all three expectations together keep their binds in order: payload, then status, then hash', () => {
64:     const payload = { secuura: { hash: 'h' } };
65:     const built = buildAnchorStatusUpdate('anchor_1', {
66:       status: 'confirmed',
67:       transactionHash: TX,
68:       expectMetadataPayload: payload,
69:       expectStatus: ['submitting', 'submitted'],
70:       expectTransactionHash: TX,
71:     }, NOW)!;
72:     expect(built.sql).toBe(
73:       'UPDATE anchor_store SET status = $1, transaction_hash = $2, simulated = $3, updated_at = $4 ' +
74:       'WHERE id = $5 AND metadata_payload = $6::jsonb AND status = ANY($7::text[]) AND transaction_hash = $8',
75:     );
76:     expect(built.params).toEqual(['confirmed', TX, false, NOW, 'anchor_1', JSON.stringify(payload), ['submitting', 'submitted'], TX]);
77:     expect(built.guarded).toBe(true);
78:     expect(built.conditional).toBe(true);
79:   });
80: });
81: 
82: function deps(over: Partial<RunStatusUpdateDeps> = {}) {
83:   const log = vi.fn();
84:   const d: RunStatusUpdateDeps = {
85:     isDbAvailable: () => true,
86:     ensureTables: vi.fn(async () => undefined),
87:     query: vi.fn(async () => ({ rowCount: 1 })),
88:     log,
89:     ...over,
90:   };
91:   return { d, log };
92: }
93: 
94: describe('(c1) KS-1384 F-a: runAnchorStatusUpdate says how many rows the write matched, or null', () => {
95:   it('a write that matched one row resolves to 1', async () => {
96:     const { d } = deps();
97:     await expect(runAnchorStatusUpdate(d, 'anchor_1', { status: 'failed' }, NOW)).resolves.toBe(1);
98:   });
99: 
100:   it('a write that matched no row resolves to 0, not null', async () => {
101:     const { d } = deps({ query: vi.fn(async () => ({ rowCount: 0 })) });
102:     await expect(runAnchorStatusUpdate(d, 'anchor_1', { status: 'failed', expectStatus: 'submitted' }, NOW)).resolves.toBe(0);
103:   });
104: 
105:   it('a statement that throws resolves to null and logs at warn (still never rethrown)', async () => {
106:     const { d, log } = deps({ query: vi.fn(async () => { throw new Error('connection reset'); }) });

### FILE AT HEAD: Blockchain/Dev/services/anchoring/src/__tests__/ks1816-reconciler-comments-match-the-code.test.ts
1: // KS-1816 (anchoring reconciler.ts): two comments said what the code does not do. The header property claimed a chain-provider error always leaves the row untouched for the next pass
2: // (past the give-up age the same error is read as absence and the row is failed). The MIN_AGE comment claimed the live loop touches updated_at as it goes (the confirmation poll writes nothing).
3: // These cells read reconciler.ts as text. R1 and R2 are red until the two comments are corrected; C1 to C3 pin the code lines the comments describe, which must not move.
4: // KS-1384 F-a changed the code R1 and C2 describe, so both were rewritten with it, in the same commit: a provider error past the give-up age no longer fails the row (KS-1810, the header
5: // now says it never fails a row), and the give-up age is read from updated_at, not created_at (KS-1808). R1 still asserts the header states what a provider error does; C2 still pins the
6: // give-up test line. R2, C1 and C3 are unchanged.
7: import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
8: import { readFileSync } from 'fs';
9: import { resolve } from 'path';
10: 
11: const SRC = readFileSync(resolve(__dirname, '..', 'reconciler.ts'), 'utf8');
12: 
13: describe('KS-1816: the two reconciler.ts comments match the code', () => {
14:   it('RED KS-1816 R1 the header property no longer claims a provider error always leaves the row untouched', () => {
15:     expect(SRC).not.toContain('error leaves the row untouched for the next pass.');
16:     // KS-1384 F-a: the header names what a pass the provider could not answer does, and no longer says it is read as absence.
17:     expect(SRC).toContain('never fails a row and resets the count');
18:     expect(SRC).not.toContain('as absence and the row is marked failed');
19:   });
20: 
21:   it('RED KS-1816 R2 the MIN_AGE comment no longer says the live loop touches updated_at as it goes', () => {
22:     expect(SRC).not.toContain('it touches updated_at as it goes');
23:     expect(SRC).toContain('writes nothing between submitted and confirmed');
24:   });
25: 
26:   it('control KS-1816 C1 the select still ages rows by updated_at', () => {
27:     expect(SRC).toContain("AND updated_at < NOW() - ($1 || ' milliseconds')::interval");
28:   });
29: 
30:   it('control KS-1816 C2 the give-up test reads updated_at (KS-1384 F-a, KS-1808), not created_at', () => {
31:     expect(SRC).toContain('const pastWindow = now() - new Date(row.updated_at).getTime() > config.giveUpMs;');
32:     expect(SRC).not.toContain('new Date(row.created_at).getTime() > config.giveUpMs');
33:   });
34:

37:   });

### FILE AT HEAD: Blockchain/Dev/services/anchoring/src/__tests__/ks1822-a-foreign-tenants-anchor-reads-as-absent.test.ts
1: /**
2:  * =============================================================================
3:  * KS-1822 — an anchor of another tenant's document reads exactly as an absent one
4:  * =============================================================================
5:  * THE DEFECT. `GET /api/anchors/:id` and `GET /api/anchors/document/:documentId` had no
6:  * tenant check: behind `jwtAuthenticate()` only, with unscoped row reads, so a caller in
7:  * tenant A read tenant B's anchor — content hash, transaction, identity block and the
8:  * KS-1454 signature hash — by id or by document id.
9:  *
10:  * THE RULE PINNED HERE (anchorReadGate.ts): the KS-1603 write gate's decision table,
11:  * applied to the reads. Refuse only a row whose document is held by a `documents` row in
12:  * another tenant, and answer that refusal with the 404 the SAME request gets when no row
13:  * exists — status, content type, length, ETag and body byte for byte — so the refusal is
14:  * not an existence oracle. Allow, by name: own tenant; no document holds the id (flat
15:  * anchors, residual i); no usable tenant claim (residual ii); probe fault (residual iii).
16:  *
17:  * WHY THIS FILE BOOTS THE REAL APP. The defect is in what the two HANDLERS answer, after
18:  * the whole middleware chain. A cell that called the gate function directly would pass
19:  * with the gate unwired. So every cell drives the real `index.ts` app (the KS-1603 /
20:  * KS-1717 recipe: `express.application.listen` is spied before the import, the app is
21:  * served on an ephemeral port) with a real RS256 bearer.
22:  *
23:  * THE DB BOUNDARY IS MOCKED, not the gate. `../db` keeps its real exports, with
24:  * `isDbAvailable` true, `query` answering the two `anchor_store` SELECTs from `h.anchors`,
25:  * and `transaction` handing the REAL `readIdOwners` a client that answers its `documents`
26:  * SELECT from `h.documents` (or throws, for the probe-fault cell). So the id resolution
27:  * the gate relies on (external_id first, then the primary key) is the real code.
28:  *
29:  * RED ON DEVELOP (no gate): R1, R2, R3 (foreign rows answered 200), R7 and R8 (no warn
30:  * line), R9 (no refusal line in the log), R11 (foreign answered 200).
31:  * CONTROLS, green on develop and after: R4, R5, R6, R10, R12 — they pin what the gate must
32:  * NOT change (own-tenant bodies, the residuals, the case-insensitive tenant compare, the
33:  * empty-read short-circuit).
34:  * =============================================================================
35:  */

56:         const keys = primaryKeys.map((k) => k.toLowerCase());

58:           .filter((d) => (d.external_id !== null && externalIds.includes(d.external_id)) || keys.includes(d.id.toLowerCase()))

153: const { privateKey, publicKey } = crypto.generateKeyPairSync('rsa', { modulusLength: 2048 });
154: const PRIVATE_PEM = privateKey.export({ type: 'pkcs8', format: 'pem' }).toString();
155: const PUBLIC_PEM = publicKey.export({ type: 'spki', format: 'pem' }).toString();
156: const b64url = (b: Buffer): string => b.toString('base64').replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '');
157: function token(claims: Record<string, unknown>): string {
158:   const header = b64url(Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ alg: 'RS256', typ: 'JWT' })));
159:   const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
160:   const payload = b64url(Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ iat: now, exp: now + 600, ...claims })));
161:   return `${header}.${payload}.${b64url(crypto.sign('RSA-SHA256', Buffer.from(`${header}.${payload}`), PRIVATE_PEM))}`;
162: }
163: const caller = (tenantId: string | undefined, organizationId: string = ORG_A): string =>
164:   token({ userId: '2a182200-0000-4000-8000-0000000018a2', role: 'user', organizationId, ...(tenantId === undefined ? {} : { tenantId }) });
165: const IN_A = (): string => caller(TENANT_A);
166: const IN_B = (): string => caller(TENANT_B, ORG_B);

168: /** Everything a client can observe about a response except the clock (Date). */
169: type Wire = { status: number; type: string | null; length: string | null; etag: string | null; body: string };
170: 
171: let server: http.Server;
172: let base = '';
173: const logLines: string[] = [];
174: 
175: async function get(path: string, tok: string): Promise<Wire> {
176:   const res = await fetch(`${base}${path}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${tok}` } });
177:   const body = await res.text();
178:   return { status: res.status, type: res.headers.get('content-type'), length: res.headers.get('content-length'), etag: res.headers.get('etag'), body };
179: }
180: /** The same request against the same app with NO rows stored — what "absent" answers. */
181: async function absent(path: string, tok: string): Promise<Wire> {
182:   const saved = h.anchors;
183:   h.anchors = [];
184:   try { return await get(path, tok); } finally { h.anchors = saved; }
185: }
186: /** index.ts `log()` writes one JSON object per console.log line; the KS-1822 ones. */

189:     .filter((l) => l.startsWith('{') && l.includes('KS-1822'))

292:       expect(lines[0].level).toBe('warn');
293:       expect(String(lines[0].message)).toContain('no usable tenant claim');
294:     }
295:   });
296: 
297:   it('RED R8 residual (iii): a probe fault ALLOWS the read (fail-open) and logs exactly one warn naming it', async () => {
298:     h.probeFault = true;
299:     const res = await get(`/api/anchors/${FOREIGN_ANCHOR}`, IN_A());
300:     expect(res.status, `received body: ${res.body}`).toBe(200);
301:     expect(JSON.parse(res.body)).toEqual(byIdBody(FOREIGN_DOC));
302:     expect(h.probes).toBeGreaterThan(0);
303:     const lines = ks1822Lines();

### FILE AT HEAD: Blockchain/Dev/services/anchoring/src/__tests__/ks584-reconciler.test.ts
1: /**
2:  * =============================================================================
3:  * KS-584 (P3) — stuck-anchor reconciler
4:  * =============================================================================
5:  * Pins the reconciler's contract:
6:  *   - a stale 'pending' row whose REAL tx is settled on-chain becomes
7:  *     'confirmed' with the block facts (the QA-High root: anchor 84fe214b…
8:  *     sat 'pending' while settled, feeding anonymous verifiers a false
9:  *     negative);
10:  *   - a real tx absent from the chain past the give-up window becomes
11:  *     terminal 'failed' (the KS-535 `All inputs are spent` class). Since
12:  *     KS-1384 F-a the window runs from the row's LAST WRITE (`updated_at`,
13:  *     KS-1808) and needs TWO consecutive passes in which the chain ANSWERED
14:  *     "not found" (KS-1810), so this cell runs two passes and the failed write
15:  *     carries the guard (the status set and hash it read). Its fake check now
16:  *     reports `polled: 1`, the answered single-shot `waitForConfirmation(tx,
17:  *     1, 0, 0)` returns, and its fake write reports the 1 row it matched;
18:  *   - a young absent tx is left alone (the live confirm loop may still win);
19:  *   - fabricated refs are never chain-checked (KS-587's rows);
20:  *   - a chain-provider error leaves the row untouched;
21:  *   - no DB / no Cardano → no-op.
22:  * =============================================================================
23:  */
24: 
25: import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
26: import { createStuckAnchorReconciler, type ReconcilerDeps } from '../reconciler';
27: 
28: const REAL_TX = '84fe214bd00257443c81e240a074763d476f9096b7e3525065bad06ec51c38c3';
29: const HOUR = 3_600_000;
30: 
31: function makeDeps(rows: any[], over: Partial<ReconcilerDeps> = {}) {
32:   const updates: Array<{ id: string; updates: Record<string, unknown> }> = [];
33:   const deps: ReconcilerDeps = {
34:     isDbAvailable: () => true,
35:     isCardanoReady: () => true,
36:     query: vi.fn(async () => ({ rows })),
37:     // KS-1384 F-a: an ANSWERED "not found" (the single-shot poll reached the chain), and a write that matched its row.
38:     checkTx: vi.fn(async () => ({ confirmed: false, polled: 1, errored: 0 })),
39:     updateStatus: vi.fn(async (id: string, u: Record<string, unknown>) => { updates.push({ id, updates: u }); return 1; }),
40:     log: vi.fn(),
41:     now: () => new Date('2026-08-11T12:00:00Z').getTime(),
42:     ...over,
43:   };
44:   return { deps, updates };
45: }
46: 
47: const CONFIG = { minAgeMs: 10 * 60_000, giveUpMs: 24 * HOUR, batch: 25 };
48: 
49: function staleRow(over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
50:   return {
51:     id: 'anchor-1',
52:     transaction_hash: REAL_TX,
53:     created_at: '2026-08-11T09:00:00Z', // 3h old — inside the give-up window
54:     updated_at: '2026-08-11T09:00:00Z',
55:     ...over,
56:   };
57: }
58: 
59: describe('KS-584 reconciler', () => {
60:   it('confirms a stale pending row whose tx is settled on-chain', async () => {
61:     const { deps, updates } = makeDeps([staleRow()], {
62:       checkTx: vi.fn(async () => ({ confirmed: true, blockNumber: 4547897, blockHash: 'bh', slot: 123, confirmations: 1 })),
63:     });
64:     const run = createStuckAnchorReconciler(deps, CONFIG);
65:     const out = await run();
66:     expect(out).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 1, failed: 0 });
67:     expect(updates[0].id).toBe('anchor-1');
68:     expect(updates[0].updates.status).toBe('confirmed');
69:     expect(updates[0].updates.blockNumber).toBe(4547897);
70:   });
71: 
72:   it('fails a real tx absent from chain past the give-up window', async () => {
73:     // KS-1384 F-a: last written 60h ago (KS-1808 reads updated_at), and two answered passes (KS-1810).
74:     const { deps, updates } = makeDeps([staleRow({ created_at: '2026-08-09T00:00:00Z', updated_at: '2026-08-09T00:00:00Z' })]);
75:     const run = createStuckAnchorReconciler(deps, CONFIG);
76:     expect(await run()).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 0 });
77:     expect(updates).toHaveLength(0);
78:     const out = await run();
79:     expect(out).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 1 });
80:     expect(updates[0].updates.status).toBe('failed');
81:     expect(String(updates[0].updates.errorMessage)).toMatch(/never appeared/);
82:     expect(updates[0].updates.expectStatus).toEqual(['pending', 'submitted', 'submitting']);
83:     expect(updates[0].updates.expectTransactionHash).toBe(REAL_TX);
84:   });
85: 
86:   it('leaves a young absent tx alone', async () => {
87:     const { deps, updates } = makeDeps([staleRow()]); // 3h old, checkTx not confirmed
88:     const run = createStuckAnchorReconciler(deps, CONFIG);
89:     const out = await run();
90:     expect(out).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 0 });
91:     expect(updates).toHaveLength(0);
92:   });
93: 
94:   it('never chain-checks fabricated refs', async () => {
95:     const checkTx = vi.fn(async () => ({ confirmed: true }));
96:     const { deps, updates } = makeDeps([staleRow({ transaction_hash: `tx_sim_${'a'.repeat(64)}` })], { checkTx });
97:     const run = createStuckAnchorReconciler(deps, CONFIG);
98:     const out = await run();
99:     expect(out.scanned).toBe(0);
100:     expect(checkTx).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
101:     expect(updates).toHaveLength(0);
102:   });
103: 
104:   it('leaves the row untouched on a chain-provider error', async () => {
105:     const { deps, updates } = makeDeps([staleRow()], {
106:       checkTx: vi.fn(async () => { throw new Error('blockfrost 429'); }),
107:     });
108:     const run = createStuckAnchorReconciler(deps, CONFIG);
109:     const out = await run();
110:     expect(out).toEqual({ scanned: 1, confirmed: 0, failed: 0 });
111:     expect(updates).toHaveLength(0);
112:   });
113: 
114:   it('no-ops without DB or Cardano', async () => {
115:     const q = vi.fn(async () => ({ rows: [] }));
116:     const { deps } = makeDeps([], { isCardanoReady: () => false, query: q });
117:     const run = createStuckAnchorReconciler(deps, CONFIG);
118:     await run();
119:     expect(q).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
120:   });
121: });

1: /**

59:   // non-mutating: contentHash stays the current blob.
