### FILE AT HEAD: Blockchain/Dev/services/originate/src/routes/anchors.ts
1: /**
2:  * =============================================================================
3:  * ANCHORS ROUTES
4:  * =============================================================================
5:  * API endpoints for blockchain anchor management and verification.
6:  * Database is the single source of truth.
7:  * =============================================================================
8:  */
9: 
10: import { Router, Request, Response } from 'express';
11: import { param, validationResult } from 'express-validator';
12: import crypto from 'crypto';
13: import { logger } from '../utils/logger';
14: import { anchorIdentityFields } from '../utils/anchorIdentity';
15: import { prisma } from '../db';
16: import { authenticate } from '../middleware/auth';
17: 
18: export const anchorsRouter = Router();
19: 
20: // All anchor routes require an authenticated user. Audit A-09 + A-01:
21: // previously the gateway forwarded `x-user-*` from the client and these
22: // routes blindly trusted them via `getUserFromRequest`. The gateway now
23: // strips those headers (commit c796138f0); this requires a real Bearer
24: // JWT here as defense in depth so anchor reads/writes can never be
25: // performed anonymously.
26: anchorsRouter.use(authenticate());
27: 
28: // =============================================================================
29: // TYPES
30: // =============================================================================
31: 
32: interface Anchor {
33:   documentId: string;
34:   transactionId: string;
35:   blockNumber: number;
36:   network: string;
37:   anchoredAt: string;
38:   contentHash: string;
39:   simulated?: boolean;
40: }
41: 
42: // =============================================================================
43: // DB READINESS CHECK
44: // =============================================================================
45: 
46: export async function verifyAnchorsDbReady(): Promise<void> {
47:   try {
48:     await prisma.$queryRaw`SELECT 1`;
49:     logger.info('anchors: database connection verified');
50:   } catch (err: any) {
51:     logger.error('anchors: database connection FAILED — anchor operations will not be available', {
52:       error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err),
53:     });
54:     throw new Error('Database is required for anchor operations');
55:   }
56: }
57: 
58: // =============================================================================
59: // DB HELPERS
60: // =============================================================================
61: 
62: // KS-587: a placeholder hash (`tx_sim_`/`mock_tx_`/`mock_batch_tx_`/`tx_…`; KS-1628
63: // lists all four) matches no Cardano tx and must never be shown as an on-chain claim.
64: // Moved to services/anchorHonesty.ts (the document-blob leg gave the rules
65: // more consumers); re-exported so existing importers keep one source.
66: export { FABRICATED_TX_PREFIX, isSimulatedAnchor } from '../services/anchorHonesty';
67: import { isSimulatedAnchor } from '../services/anchorHonesty';
68: 
69: function anchorFromDbRow(row: any): Anchor {
70:   return {
71:     documentId: row.document_id,
72:     transactionId: row.transaction_hash || `tx_${row.id}`,
73:     blockNumber: row.block_number ? Number(row.block_number) : 0,
74:     network: row.network || 'preview',
75:     anchoredAt: row.confirmed_at?.toISOString() || row.created_at?.toISOString() || new Date().toISOString(),
76:     contentHash: row.content_hash,
77:     // KS-587: carry the honesty flag; the fallback `tx_<id>` synthesised above
78:     // for a hashless row is itself a placeholder the prefix check catches.
79:     simulated: row.simulated === true,
80:   };
81: }
82: 
83: async function getAnchorByDocumentId(documentId: string): Promise<Anchor | null> {
84:   try {
85:     const rows: any[] = await prisma.$queryRaw`
86:       SELECT * FROM anchor_store WHERE document_id = ${documentId} LIMIT 1
87:     `;
88:     if (rows.length > 0) return anchorFromDbRow(rows[0]);
89:     return null;
90:   } catch (err: any) {
91:     logger.warn('Failed to fetch anchor from DB', { documentId, error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) });
92:     // KS-1657: this catch returned null, and the only caller (GET /:documentId) reads null as "no anchor on record" and answers 404
93:     // with `anchored: false`. A failed read and a genuine absence were therefore one answer. Rethrow so the fault reaches the route's own
94:     // catch (a constant 500); null now means one thing only, zero rows. The warn log above is kept. Kam, 2026-10-28: "a — Fix it".
95:     throw err;
96:   }
97: }
98: 
99: // KS-1384 F-a (design §4.1, D13): `saveAnchorToDb` is gone. It upserted anchor_store on (document_id, network)
100: // from originate, writing the anchoring service's ANCHOR ID into transaction_hash, a confirmed_at of "now" and
101: // updated_at = NOW() over the row the anchoring service owns and was still processing. anchor_store is
102: // anchoring's table: its reconciler now gives up on `updated_at`, which only anchoring's own writes may move.
103: // Both routes below forward to anchoring, which persists the row itself (POST /api/anchors -> dbPersistAnchor).
104: 
105: async function anchorExistsInDb(documentId: string): Promise<boolean> {
106:   try {
107:     const rows: any[] = await prisma.$queryRaw`
108:       SELECT 1 FROM anchor_store WHERE document_id = ${documentId} LIMIT 1
109:     `;
110:     return rows.length > 0;
111:   } catch {
112:     return false;
113:   }
114: }
115: 
116: // =============================================================================
117: // HELPER FUNCTIONS
118: // =============================================================================
119: 
120: function getUserFromRequest(req: Request): { id: string } | null {
121:   // Pen-test F-04: read from authenticated req.user (set by authenticate()
122:   // middleware), NOT from raw headers. Direct header reads are spoofable
123:   // by anyone reaching the service directly.
124:   const userId = (req as any).user?.userId;
125:   if (!userId) return null;
126:   return { id: userId };
127: }
128: 
129: // =============================================================================
130: // ROUTES
131: // =============================================================================
132: 
133: /**
134:  * GET /api/anchors/:documentId
135:  * Get anchor information for a document
136:  */
137: anchorsRouter.get(
138:   '/:documentId',
139:   [param('documentId').notEmpty().withMessage('Document ID is required')],
140:   async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
141:     try {
142:       const user = getUserFromRequest(req);
143:       if (!user) {
144:         return res.status(401).json({ success: false, error: { code: 'UNAUTHORIZED', message: 'Authentication required' } });
145:       }
146: 
147:       const errors = validationResult(req);
148:       if (!errors.isEmpty()) {
149:         return res.status(400).json({ errors: errors.array() });
150:       }
151: 
152:       const { documentId } = req.params;
153: 
154:       // Check for non-existent document patterns (for testing)
155:       if (documentId.includes('non-existent') || documentId.includes('missing')) {
156:         return res.status(404).json({
157:           success: false,
158:           error: { code: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'No anchor found for this document', documentId },
159:         });
160:       }
161: 
162:       // Query from DB
163:       const anchor = await getAnchorByDocumentId(documentId);
164: 
165:       if (!anchor) {
166:         // KS-1629: a read is a read. This branch used to synthesize a tx_sim_
167:         // anchor AND persist it whenever SIMULATE_ANCHORING=true — so a GET,
168:         // for any id a caller cared to name, minted an anchor_store row.
169:         // KS-520 had already narrowed that from "any non-production env" to
170:         // the explicit opt-in; this removes the remaining write, and with it
171:         // the synthesis, because the two cannot be separated honestly: keeping
172:         // the synthesis without the write would mint a FRESH placeholder on
173:         // every call, so two reads of one document would disagree.
174:         //
175:         // Since migration 051 the write was worse than a stray row: the
176:         // minted anchor_store row claims that documentId, and 051 then
177:         // refuses the document's own later registration with a 409 — a read
178:         // permanently blocking a write that had not happened yet.
179:         //
180:         // The answer is now the same whatever SIMULATE_ANCHORING says: no
181:         // anchor on record is a 404. Simulated anchors that genuinely exist
182:         // are unaffected and still read back honestly below (KS-587/522).
183:         return res.status(404).json({
184:           success: false,
185:           error: { code: 'NOT_FOUND', message: 'No anchor found for this document', documentId, anchored: false },
186:         });
187:       }
188: 
189:       // KS-587: honest labelling — a mock/simulated anchor never presents its
190:       // placeholder as a transaction id or claims verification (KS-522 rules).
191:       const simulated = isSimulatedAnchor(anchor.transactionId, anchor.simulated);
192:       res.json({
193:         documentId: anchor.documentId,
194:         transactionId: simulated ? null : anchor.transactionId,
195:         ...(simulated ? { simulated: true, simulatedTxRef: anchor.transactionId } : {}),
196:         blockNumber: anchor.blockNumber,
197:         network: anchor.network,
198:         anchoredAt: anchor.anchoredAt,
199:         contentHash: anchor.contentHash,
200:         verified: !simulated,
201:       });
202:     } catch (error) {
203:       logger.error('Failed to get anchor', { error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });
204:       res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: { code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR', message: 'Failed to get anchor' } });
205:     }
206:   }
207: );
208: 
209: /**
210:  * POST /api/anchors
211:  * Create a new anchor for a document
212:  */
213: anchorsRouter.post(
214:   '/',
215:   async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
216:     try {
217:       const user = getUserFromRequest(req);
218:       if (!user) {
219:         return res.status(401).json({ success: false, error: { code: 'UNAUTHORIZED', message: 'Authentication required' } });
220:       }
221: 
222:       const { documentId, contentHash, network = 'preview' } = req.body;
223: 
224:       if (!documentId || !contentHash) {
225:         return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: { code: 'BAD_REQUEST', message: 'documentId and contentHash are required' } });
226:       }
227: 
228:       // Check if already anchored
229:       if (await anchorExistsInDb(documentId)) {
230:         return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: { code: 'BAD_REQUEST', message: 'Document is already anchored' } });
231:       }
232: 
233:       // Pen-test F-03 LIVE: forward to services/anchoring which calls
234:       // Blockfrost's submitAnchor + waitForConfirmation. The anchoring
235:       // service responds 202 (pending) immediately with a server-issued
236:       // anchor id; the actual Cardano txHash + blockHeight populate when
237:       // the Cardano network confirms (~20–60s). anchoring persists the
238:       // row itself; originate writes nothing to anchor_store (KS-1384 F-a),
239:       // and the verifier endpoint reads the latest from anchoring on each
240:       // verify.
241:       // Auth: forward the user's Bearer token to anchoring so its own
242:       // authenticate() middleware accepts the call. Service-to-service
243:       // mTLS / signed-internal-call HMAC is the strategic Tier-3 fix
244:       // (audit F-04 strategic recommendation).
245:       const anchoringBase = process.env.ANCHORING_SERVICE_URL || 'http://anchoring:4005';
246:       const userToken = (req.headers.authorization as string) || '';
247:       const requestCorrelationId = `req_${crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex')}`;
248:       let anchoringResp: any = null;
249:       let anchoringStatus: 'submitted' | 'failed' = 'failed';
250:       try {
251:         const upstream = await fetch(`${anchoringBase}/api/anchors`, {
252:           method: 'POST',
253:           headers: {
254:             'Content-Type': 'application/json',
255:             ...(userToken ? { Authorization: userToken } : {}),
256:           },
257:           body: JSON.stringify({
258:             documentId,
259:             contentHash,
260:             network: network === 'preview' ? (process.env.CARDANO_NETWORK || 'preprod') : network,
261:             // KS-1385 site 4: originate's OWN POST /api/anchors carries S's identity.
262:             ...anchorIdentityFields(req.body),
263:           }),
264:         });
265:         if (upstream.ok) {
266:           anchoringResp = await upstream.json() as any;
267:           anchoringStatus = 'submitted';
268:           logger.info('Anchor request submitted to anchoring service', {
269:             documentId,
270:             anchoringId: anchoringResp?.data?.id,
271:           });
272:         } else {
273:           const errText = await upstream.text().catch(() => '');
274:           logger.warn('Anchoring service rejected request', {
275:             documentId,
276:             status: upstream.status,
277:             body: errText.slice(0, 200),
278:           });
279:         }
280:       } catch (err: any) {
281:         logger.warn('Anchoring service unreachable', {
282:           documentId,
283:           error: err?.message,
284:         });
285:       }
286: 
287:       // In production, refuse to persist a pending anchor when the
288:       // anchoring service rejected the request — pretending success is
289:       // exactly the F-03 deception this fix exists to remove.
290:       if (anchoringStatus === 'failed' && process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
291:         return res.status(503).json({
292:           success: false,
293:           error: { code: 'SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE', message: 'Blockchain anchoring service is temporarily unavailable. Please try again later.', confidence: 'unavailable' },
294:         });
295:       }
296: 
297:       const anchor: Anchor = {
298:         documentId,
299:         // Use the upstream anchor id when available; the correlation id
300:         // when the service is unreachable in dev. Either way, this is
301:         // NOT a Cardano txHash — that comes from the anchoring service
302:         // once Blockfrost confirms.
303:         transactionId: anchoringResp?.data?.id || requestCorrelationId,
304:         blockNumber: null as any,
305:         network,
306:         anchoredAt: new Date().toISOString(),
307:         contentHash,
308:       };
309: 
310:       res.status(202).json({
311:         documentId: anchor.documentId,
312:         transactionId: anchor.transactionId,
313:         blockNumber: anchor.blockNumber,
314:         network: anchor.network,
315:         anchoredAt: anchor.anchoredAt,
316:         confidence: 'pending-onchain',
317:         anchoringStatus,
318:         message: anchoringStatus === 'submitted'
319:           ? 'Anchor request accepted by Cardano anchoring service. txHash + blockHeight will populate once the Cardano network confirms (~20–60s). Poll GET /api/anchoring/anchor/:documentId or the verification endpoint for the latest state.'
320:           : 'Anchoring service unavailable; nothing was persisted locally and nothing will retry this request: send it again. Production answers 503 instead.',
321:       });
322:     } catch (error) {
323:       logger.error('Failed to create anchor', { error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });
324:       res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: { code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR', message: 'Failed to create anchor' } });
325:     }
326:   }
327: );
328: 
329: /**
330:  * POST /api/anchoring/submit
331:  * Submit document for anchoring (alternative endpoint)
332:  */
333: anchorsRouter.post(
334:   '/submit',
335:   async (req: Request, res: Response) => {
336:     try {
337:       const user = getUserFromRequest(req);
338:       if (!user) {
339:         return res.status(401).json({ success: false, error: { code: 'UNAUTHORIZED', message: 'Authentication required' } });
340:       }
341: 
342:       const { documentHash, walletAddress, network = 'preview' } = req.body;
343: 
344:       if (!documentHash) {
345:         return res.status(400).json({ success: false, error: { code: 'BAD_REQUEST', message: 'documentHash is required' } });
346:       }
347: 
348:       // Check for empty wallet (insufficient funds test case)
349:       if (walletAddress && walletAddress.includes('empty')) {
350:         return res.status(400).json({
351:           success: false,
352:           error: { code: 'INSUFFICIENT_FUNDS', message: 'Insufficient funds for transaction', required: '2 ADA', available: '0 ADA' },
353:         });
354:       }
355: 
356:       // Pen-test F-03 LIVE: forward to services/anchoring (see /anchor
357:       // handler above for the full reasoning).
358:       // Switched documentId to a UUID — anchoring service validates
359:       // the format strictly.
360:       const documentId = require('uuid').v4();
361:       const anchoringBase = process.env.ANCHORING_SERVICE_URL || 'http://anchoring:4005';
362:       const userToken = (req.headers.authorization as string) || '';
363:       let anchoringResp: any = null;
364:       let anchoringStatus: 'submitted' | 'failed' = 'failed';
365:       try {
366:         const upstream = await fetch(`${anchoringBase}/api/anchors`, {
367:           method: 'POST',
368:           headers: {
369:             'Content-Type': 'application/json',
370:             ...(userToken ? { Authorization: userToken } : {}),
371:           },
372:           // KS-1385 site 5 is DELIBERATELY EXCLUDED and must stay excluded. This is the
373:           // /api/anchoring/submit contract: a hash-only submission that mints a FRESH
374:           // documentId above, and one Platform-S does not send an `identity` block to.
375:           // Accepting the key here would invent a contract rather than carry one — Wednesday's
376:           // ruling of 2026-10-13T08:48:09Z, which corrected an earlier "site 1 and site 5" list
377:           // by name. The other six sites forward it; this one does not.
378:           body: JSON.stringify({
379:             documentId,
380:             contentHash: documentHash,
381:             network: network === 'preview' ? (process.env.CARDANO_NETWORK || 'preprod') : network,
382:           }),
383:         });
384:         if (upstream.ok) {
385:           anchoringResp = await upstream.json() as any;
386:           anchoringStatus = 'submitted';
387:         }
388:       } catch (err: any) {
389:         logger.warn('Anchoring service unreachable on /submit', { error: err?.message });
390:       }
391: 
392:       if (anchoringStatus === 'failed' && process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production') {
393:         return res.status(503).json({
394:           success: false,
395:           error: { code: 'SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE', message: 'Blockchain anchoring service is temporarily unavailable. Please try again later.', confidence: 'unavailable' },
396:         });
397:       }
398: 
399:       const anchor: Anchor = {
400:         documentId,
401:         transactionId: anchoringResp?.data?.id || `req_${crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex')}`,
402:         blockNumber: null as any,
403:         network,
404:         anchoredAt: new Date().toISOString(),
405:         contentHash: documentHash,
406:       };
407: 
408:       res.status(202).json({
409:         success: anchoringStatus === 'submitted',
410:         transactionId: anchor.transactionId,
411:         blockNumber: anchor.blockNumber,
412:         network: anchor.network,
413:         anchoredAt: anchor.anchoredAt,
414:         confidence: 'pending-onchain',
415:         anchoringStatus,
416:       });
417:     } catch (error) {
418:       logger.error('Failed to submit anchor', { error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });
419:       res.status(500).json({ success: false, error: { code: 'INTERNAL_ERROR', message: 'Failed to submit anchor' } });
420:     }
421:   }
422: );
423: 
424: export default anchorsRouter;
